# M3-C 规则引擎 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 把 spec §10 的规则体系落成能跑的一层——规则入库、模板纯求值、三级触发、冷却去重、命中后经 M3-B 那条路投递出去，并补上 §9.2 的建仓派生规则与 §9.3 的周复检。

**Architecture:** 三层各管一件事，边界由分层守卫钉住：`core/rules.py` 只有词汇（模板名单、参数表、阶段、`Verdict`），`storage/repos/rules.py` 只有写入与修订史，求值与装配在 `services/rules/`（纯函数模板 + 一份取数装配 + 一趟引擎）。引擎**不认识** `monitor`：投递是一个注入的端口（`core/notify.py::AlertSender`），`bootstrap` 把 `NotifyService.dispatch` 接上去——这是 D93 已经验证过的形状（`HealthService` 收 `local_status` 而不是 import `monitor`）。

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13、DuckDB、Polars、pytest；无新第三方依赖（`GATED` 名单不变）。

---

## 0. 执行者必须先读的六条硬约束

这个仓库有几条规矩是**违反了就白干**的，写在这里而不是散落在各任务里：

1. **只用 `uv run`**。`uv run pytest …`、`uv run ruff check …`、`uv run sams …`。系统 Python 里没有装依赖。
2. **lint 只跑 `uv run ruff check src tests`，永远不跑 `ruff format`**。行宽 100，规则集 E,F,I,UP,B,SIM。ruff 偶尔发疯就加 `--no-cache`。
3. **绝不修改已应用的迁移**。`schema_migrations.checksum` 会在下一次 `sams db migrate` 时当场拒绝。要动表结构就新开一个 `00xx_*.sql`。
4. **每个任务结束时先合并远端再推送**：`git fetch origin main && git merge --ff-only origin/main && git push origin main`。永远不 `--force`、不 `--amend` 去让一次推送成功——推不上去就把那一次失败原样报出来，它不把任务算失败。测试用的库只能是 `tmp/` 下的副本，绝不写 `data/sams.duckdb`。
5. **真实网络的用例打 `@pytest.mark.live`**，日常跑 `-m "not live"`。
6. **屏幕与 `--json` 成对**（D57）：每条命令拒绝时也要给 JSON，`exit` 键与真正的退出码同值。`exit_for`：`ValidationError` ⇒ 2，其余 ⇒ 1。

**先量一遍水位**（每一步都该是绿的，红了就退回上一个任务，不许「先跳过」）：

```bash
uv run ruff check src tests --no-cache
uv run pytest tests/test_layering.py -q
uv run pytest -m "not live" -q          # 当前基线：2195 passed / 14 deselected
```

## 1. 这一层要防的是哪一类错

这一层的错**全部长成「屏幕上看着正常」的样子**，所以每个任务的测试断言写的都是「这里必须是这句话」，不是「这里不崩」：

| 错法 | 屏幕上什么样 | 真实的代价 |
|---|---|---|
| 除权日误报跌破止损 | 一条完全合理的「止损已破，请立即处置」 | 用户在一次分红那天被指挥去卖一笔没亏的仓（§10.2 的强制验收用例） |
| 冷却把升级吞掉 | 「已压制」，屏幕上那一行写着 cooldown | `pre` 报了之后 30 分钟内的 `hit`（真跌破）没人听见——冷却是给降噪用的，不是给止损用的（D94） |
| 跨账户互相覆盖 | 只有一条告警 | 同一只票两个账户不同成本，A 触发后 B 的破位被当成重复丢掉（§10.1 的去重键含 `account_id`） |
| 周复检把「从没比过」说成「没漂移」 | 「观点未漂移」 | 基线没建立时那是一句没人验证过的谎（D96） |
| 阈值没带币种 | `止损 100` | A 股是元、美股是美元；§5.4 规则 3 禁止未标注币种的金额 |
| 规则的 `channel_ids` 指向别人的通道 | 发出去了，发到别人群里 | 归属必须在服务层核过一次，仓库只保证「这条通道属于这个用户」 |

## 2. 文件结构（谁负责什么）

```
src/sams/core/rules.py                  词汇：STAGES / STAGE_RANK / TARGET_TYPES / Param /
                                        Template / TEMPLATES / RULE_KINDS / validate_params /
                                        FILTER_KEYS / validate_filters / Verdict / DRIFT_TIER
src/sams/storage/repos/rules.py         RuleRepo：写入、修订史、启停、删除闸、归属查询
src/sams/storage/migrations/0016_rule_baseline.sql
                                        theses 的三项基线（分数、分项、属于哪天）
src/sams/storage/migrations/0017_rule_params_on.sql
                                        rules.params_on：那一屏参数属于哪一天的价空间
src/sams/services/_fold.py              折回现价口径（估值与规则共用同一件，D73 的算式只写一遍）
src/sams/services/rules/__init__.py     转出引擎、取数、模板、过滤、约束文案那一组名字
                                        （维护面的 `RuleService` 在 `rules.maintenance`，
                                        `bootstrap` 与命令行都按模块路径取它）
src/sams/services/rules/snapshot.py     唯一的取数处：expand(rule) → 一组 Target，read(...) → Snapshot
src/sams/services/rules/anchor.py       除权换算：把人写下来的价搬进今天的价空间（§10.2）
src/sams/services/rules/templates.py    纯函数：每个 kind 一个 evaluate，TABLE 挂名字
src/sams/services/rules/filters.py      纯函数：四个过滤开关（§10.1 的降噪主力）
src/sams/services/rules/notes.py        纯函数：执行约束文案（T+1 / 跌停 / 保证金 / 币种）
src/sams/services/rules/engine.py       RuleEngine：一趟、冷却与升级、投递、事件
src/sams/services/rules/derive.py       §9.2 建仓派生 + 参数回写 thesis
src/sams/services/rules/recheck.py      §9.3 周复检（基线建立、掉档判定）
src/sams/services/rules/maintenance.py  RuleService：维护面（归属、通道核、拒绝也要能解释）
src/sams/cli/rule.py                    `sams rule kinds|add|list|show|edit|enable|disable|remove|derive|check|recheck`
src/sams/cli/alert.py                   `sams alert list|show|handle|dismiss`
```

测试镜像在 `tests/` 同名目录下：`tests/core/test_rules_contract.py`、`tests/core/test_notify_alert_sender.py`、`tests/storage/test_rules_repo.py`、`tests/services/test_fold.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_snapshot.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_anchor.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_templates_stops.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_templates_thesis.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_filters.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_notes.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_engine.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_derive.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_maintenance.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_ex_rights.py`、`tests/cli/test_rule_cli.py`、`tests/cli/test_alert_cli.py`、`tests/test_migration_0016.py`、`tests/test_migration_0017.py`、`tests/test_adversarial_rules.py`。

两个支援模块不是「谁的私有夹具」：`tests/support_rules.py`（`Harness`/`StubData`/`RecordingSender`/`StoringSender`/`snap`）被引擎、对抗面与命令行三头共用，`tests/support_valuation.py` 的 `seed_calendar` 被 T-18 的 CLI 用例与 T-25 的真链验收共用。改它们等于改所有引用方，所以每一步搬完都要跑一遍引用它们的文件。

## 3. 这一轮明确不做什么（写下来才不算漏）

spec §10.3 自己说了「40 个是能力目录，不是 M3 的实现承诺。M3 只实现被真实使用验证过的子集」。裁剪：

- **`market_rel` 过滤器不做**：它要一个指数序列（沪深300），而 `bars` 里没有指数、采集链也没有指数端点。`validate_filters` 会点名「`market_rel` 没有输入」。指数数据记为 U27。
- **`cli rules replay` 不做**：重放是回测的前置（M5）。这一轮只给 `sams rule check --as-of <日>`——它按那一天重算**判断**，不重放事件流，也不改冷却状态（冷却只读 `alerts`，`--as-of` 那趟不落库除非真发）。
- **组合级指标不做**（仓位占比、当日盈亏、最大回撤、板块集中度，§10.1 末段）：后三项需要每日组合快照，而库里没有那张表。持仓类规则这一轮只吃 `PortfolioService.positions` 已有的数。记为 U28。
- **盘中轮询不做**：`QuoteUpdated` 没有生产者（M3-D 的调度器才有）。`sams rule check` 是**同步一趟**，吃日线收盘。`urgent`（跳空低开破位）因此只能按当日**开盘价 vs 前收盘**判，报出来的时刻是收盘后——这一点在 §10.2 的验收里必须写清楚，M3-D 接上快照源后同一个判据会自己变早。
- **`condition_kind='expression'` 不做**（M5），`condition_kind` 列恒为 `'template'`，仓库层拒收别的值。
- **§10.3 表里其余 ~26 个价格/指标/量能模板不做**：九个止损止盈（§10.2 点名的专项）+ 五个其余 kind（`price.cross_below` / `price.cross_above` / `thesis.odds_below` / `thesis.drift` / `scheduled.reevaluate`）已经覆盖「建仓 → 持有 → 了结」这条主线，剩下的等 M3-D 挂机跑一个真实交易日之后按真实使用量补。

---

## 阶段 A：词汇与入库（T-1 ~ T-4）

### T-1：`core/rules.py`——模板表是名单的唯一出处

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/core/rules.py`
- Test: `tests/core/test_rules_contract.py`

设计约束先说清，后面的任务都靠它：

- **模板表住在 `core`**，因为三处都要读它而三处互相不许 import：`storage/repos/rules.py` 要在写入前校验 `kind`/`params`（`storage` 只许 import `core`）、`services/rules/templates.py` 按它求值、`cli/rule.py` 按它解释参数并打印帮助。把名单写进 `services` 就等于让仓库层够不着它——仓库够不着校验，垃圾就能进库（M3-B 的 `KINDS` 是同一个决定的另一处落点，见 D92 的注释）。
- **`stop.*`/`tp.*` 必须能出三档**（`stages=True`），其余 kind 恒 `hit`。`Template.severity` 是**默认级别**，规则上可以覆盖（`rules.severity` 那一列）。
- **参数只有三种形状**：数（`number`）、枚举（`choice`）、分档串（`tiers`，只有 `tp.tiered` 用）。第四种（日期）不需要：`stop.time` 吃的是「天数」这个数。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败用例** —— `tests/core/test_rules_contract.py`

```python
"""规则词汇表的行为契约（spec §10）。

这一整个模块没有 IO，所以用例就是在问「名单、默认值、拒绝的话」这三件事。
写在这里的东西会被仓库层、求值层、命令行三层各自读一遍——一处写错，三处口径分叉。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.core.rules import (
    DRIFT_TIER,
    FILTER_KEYS,
    HIT,
    PRE,
    RULE_KINDS,
    STAGE_RANK,
    STAGES,
    URGENT,
    Verdict,
    escalates,
    template_for,
    tier_of,
    validate_filters,
    validate_params,
)


def test_stage_order_is_pre_hit_urgent() -> None:
    """三档的次序只写一遍：冷却放行靠它（D94），文案里那一列也靠它。"""
    assert STAGES == ("pre", "hit", "urgent")
    assert STAGE_RANK[PRE] < STAGE_RANK[HIT] < STAGE_RANK[URGENT]


def test_every_kind_has_a_template_and_a_default_severity() -> None:
    for kind in RULE_KINDS:
        built = template_for(kind)
        assert built.kind == kind
        assert built.severity in ("strong", "mid", "weak")
        assert built.label, kind


def test_the_nine_stops_are_all_present() -> None:
    """§10.2 点名的九种，一种都不许漏：漏一种就是「止损体系完成」这句话是假的。"""
    expected = {
        "stop.fixed", "stop.pct", "stop.trailing", "stop.atr", "stop.ma",
        "stop.breakeven", "stop.time", "stop.thesis_drift", "tp.tiered",
    }
    assert expected <= set(RULE_KINDS)


def test_position_kinds_refuse_to_lose_their_account() -> None:
    """持仓类模板必须自己声明「我要挂在账户上」（§10.1 的去重键含 account_id）。"""
    assert template_for("stop.pct").needs_position is True
    assert template_for("price.cross_below").needs_position is False
    assert template_for("thesis.odds_below").needs_thesis is True


def test_an_unknown_kind_names_the_whole_list() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="stop.fixed"):
        template_for("stop.everything")


def test_a_missing_required_param_is_named_by_name() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="pct"):
        validate_params("stop.pct", {})


def test_defaults_are_filled_in_rather_than_left_absent() -> None:
    """求值层因此可以假定参数齐备：每个模板函数自己判默认值，就会有九种默认写法。"""
    assert validate_params("stop.atr", {}) == {"multiple": 2.0}


def test_a_number_out_of_range_is_refused_with_the_offending_value() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="1.5"):
        validate_params("stop.pct", {"pct": 1.5})


def test_a_stray_param_is_refused_instead_of_being_silently_ignored() -> None:
    """静默丢掉一个键，用户以为「止损比例 8%」生效了，而库里那条规则用的是默认值。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="percent"):
        validate_params("stop.pct", {"pct": 0.08, "percent": 8})


def test_a_choice_param_only_takes_the_listed_values() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="daily_ma60"):
        validate_params("stop.ma", {"basis": "hourly"})
    assert validate_params("stop.ma", {}) == {"basis": "weekly_ma20"}


def test_tiers_must_increase_and_end_at_the_target() -> None:
    assert validate_params("tp.tiered", {}) == {"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="递增"):
        validate_params("tp.tiered", {"tiers": "1.0,0.5"})
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="递增"):
        validate_params("tp.tiered", {"tiers": "0.5,0.5"})
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="有限数"):
        validate_params("tp.tiered", {"tiers": "0.5,nan"})


def test_a_non_numeric_param_says_what_it_expected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="数字"):
        validate_params("stop.fixed", {"price": "十块"})
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="有限数"):
        validate_params("stop.fixed", {"price": "nan"})  # nan 与任何线比都是 False


def test_validation_accepts_its_own_output() -> None:
    """仓库层 update 会把读回来的 `params_json` 再校验一遍：自己的输出必须吃得下。"""
    once = validate_params("tp.tiered", {})
    assert validate_params("tp.tiered", once) == once


def test_filters_are_boolean_switches_from_a_closed_list() -> None:
    assert FILTER_KEYS == ("time_window", "data_freshness", "exclude_halt", "no_open_alert")
    # 返回的是补齐了四个默认值的整张表，不是一个只含被打开开关的稀疏字典
    assert validate_filters({"data_freshness": True}) == {
        **dict.fromkeys(FILTER_KEYS, False),
        "data_freshness": True,
    }
    assert validate_filters({}) == dict.fromkeys(FILTER_KEYS, False)


def test_market_rel_is_refused_because_it_has_no_input() -> None:
    """指数序列这一层根本没有：宁可不给这个开关，也不给一个恒真的过滤器。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="指数"):
        validate_filters({"market_rel": True})


def test_an_unknown_filter_names_the_four() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="no_open_alert"):
        validate_filters({"weekend": True})


def test_a_filter_value_must_be_a_bool() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="真或假"):
        validate_filters({"time_window": "yes"})


def test_a_verdict_carries_both_sides_of_the_comparison() -> None:
    built = Verdict(
        stage=HIT, value=9.8, threshold=10.0, value_text="9.80 元", threshold_text="10 元"
    )
    assert built.ok is True
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="pre"):
        Verdict(stage="maybe", value=1.0, threshold=2.0, value_text="1", threshold_text="2")


def test_drift_tier_is_twenty_points() -> None:
    assert DRIFT_TIER == 20.0
    # 档位是 floor(总分 / 20)：100 分落在第 5 档（0~5 六档），不是封顶在第 4 档
    assert (tier_of(78.0), tier_of(61.0), tier_of(100.0)) == (3, 3, 5)
    assert tier_of(None) is None


def test_escalation_is_a_strict_rank_increase() -> None:
    """D94 的那道闸：同档与降档都不穿冷却，只有更响的一档穿。"""
    assert escalates(None, PRE) is True
    assert escalates(PRE, HIT) is True
    assert escalates(HIT, URGENT) is True
    assert escalates(HIT, HIT) is False
    assert escalates(URGENT, HIT) is False
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="bogus"):
        escalates(PRE, "bogus")
```

  注意这里断言的是 `STAGE_RANK` 而不是 `PRE < HIT < URGENT`：字符串序是 `"hit" < "pre" < "urgent"`，跟语义正好相反（`h` 在 `p` 前）。把次序写进那张表，冷却放行才有唯一读法；这一枚就是钉住那张表，而不是拿字典序当语义用。

- [ ] **Step 2：跑，确认红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/core/test_rules_contract.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError`/`ImportError: cannot import name 'DRIFT_TIER'`（模块还不存在）

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/core/rules.py`

```python
"""规则的词汇表：模板名单、参数表、阶段、过滤开关、判定形状（spec §10）。

和 `core/notify.py` 同一个理由：**不含 IO**。仓库层要按它校验写入、求值层按它取默认值、
命令行按它解释参数与打印帮助，三层都能引用而互不 import。名单一旦写进 `services`，
`storage` 就够不着它——够不着校验的名单等于没有名单，垃圾能直接进库。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from sams.core.errors import ValidationError

#: 三级触发（§10.2）。`pre` 是「快到了」，`hit` 是「破了」，`urgent` 是「跳空破的」。
STAGES = ("pre", "hit", "urgent")
PRE, HIT, URGENT = STAGES
#: 升级次序。它是「这次命中该不该被冷却吞掉」的判据（D94），所以只能有一处。
#: 不要拿字符串比：`"pre" < "hit"` 是字典序，跟语义正好相反。
STAGE_RANK = {PRE: 0, HIT: 1, URGENT: 2}

#: 规则挂在什么对象上。`target_ref` 的形状按类型定死，见 `storage/repos/rules.py`。
TARGET_TYPES = ("symbol", "watchlist", "thesis", "position", "portfolio")

#: 周复检「掉一档」的那一档有多大（§9.3 说的是「下滑超过一档」，而分是 0~100）。
#: 20 分切成五档是这一层的决定，不是分析层的：分项分的刻度只有 M2-D 那一张表知道，
#: 而「掉一档」必须是一个能复算的数，不能是排版上的形容词。
DRIFT_TIER = 20.0

#: `time_window` 那个开关的窗口：开盘前后各 5 分钟内不推价格类（集合竞价噪音，§10.1）
FILTER_MINUTES = 5
#: `data_freshness` 认的行情日落后天数。3 个日历日盖住一个周末 + 一天的数据延迟，
#: 再长就该由 `exclude_halt` 或健康页说话，而不是让规则拿三天前的价报「跌破止损」
STALE_PRICE_DAYS = 3

#: 四个可组合开关。`market_rel` **不在这里**：它要指数序列，而这一层没有那个输入（U27）。
FILTER_KEYS = ("time_window", "data_freshness", "exclude_halt", "no_open_alert")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Param:
    """模板要的一个参数。`shape` 只有三种：数、枚举、分档串。"""

    name: str
    label: str
    shape: str = "number"
    default: Any = None
    required: bool = False
    minimum: float | None = None
    maximum: float | None = None
    choices: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    unit: str = ""

    def describe(self) -> str:
        tail = f"，默认 {self.default}" if not self.required else "，必填"
        bounds = ""
        if self.minimum is not None and self.maximum is not None:
            bounds = f"（{self.minimum:g}~{self.maximum:g}{self.unit}）"
        elif self.minimum is not None:
            bounds = f"（> {self.minimum:g}{self.unit}）"
        if self.choices:
            return f"{self.name}={self.label}{'/'.join(self.choices)}{tail}"
        return f"{self.name}={self.label}{bounds}{tail}"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Template:
    """一个 kind 的形状：要哪些参数、默认什么级别、会不会出三档、挂在什么对象上。"""

    kind: str
    label: str
    params: tuple[Param, ...] = ()
    severity: str = "mid"
    #: 会产出多个档位（止损类出 pre/hit/urgent；`tp.tiered` 出 pre/hit——
    #: 中间档算「快到了」，到目标价才是那件事。这一条不只是措辞：同一去重键上
    #: 只有更响的一档能穿冷却（D94），两档都记 `hit` 会让 100% 那一档被 50% 吞掉）
    stages: bool = False
    #: 必须挂账户（去重键含 `account_id` 的那一类，§10.1）
    needs_position: bool = False
    #: 必须挂一条论（读它的价位/赔率/分项）
    needs_thesis: bool = False
    #: 价格类：开盘前后那 5 分钟窗口只作用于它们
    price_class: bool = True
    #: 允许的 target_type（写成元组而不是全放开：一条 `stop.pct` 挂在整个自选股清单上
    #: 会读成「每个账户的每笔持仓」，而它得先知道自己挂的是谁的仓）
    targets: tuple[str, ...] = TARGET_TYPES

    def describe(self) -> str:
        parts = [p.describe() for p in self.params]
        return f"{self.kind}（{self.label}）：" + ("；".join(parts) if parts else "无参数")


def _number(name: str, label: str, *, default=None, required=False, minimum=None,
            maximum=None, unit: str = "") -> Param:
    return Param(name=name, label=label, shape="number", default=default, required=required,
                 minimum=minimum, maximum=maximum, unit=unit)


def _choice(name: str, label: str, choices: tuple[str, ...], default: str) -> Param:
    return Param(name=name, label=label, shape="choice", default=default, choices=choices)


#: 每个 kind 一行。`stop.*` 的严重级别都是 strong：止损是「现在就要动」的事，
#: 把它配成 mid 就等于让默认路由绕开语音通道（§11 的 DEFAULT_ROUTE）。
TEMPLATES: dict[str, Template] = {
    "stop.fixed": Template(
        "stop.fixed", "跌破绝对价即报",
        (_number("price", "止损价", required=True, minimum=0.0, unit="元"),),
        severity="strong", stages=True, targets=("symbol", "watchlist", "thesis", "position"),
    ),
    "stop.pct": Template(
        "stop.pct", "成本下浮 x%",
        (_number("pct", "回撤比例", required=True, minimum=0.0, maximum=0.9),),
        severity="strong", stages=True, needs_position=True,
        targets=("position", "thesis"),
    ),
    "stop.trailing": Template(
        "stop.trailing", "持仓期最高价回撤 x%",
        (_number("pct", "回撤比例", required=True, minimum=0.0, maximum=0.9),),
        severity="strong", stages=True, needs_position=True, targets=("position", "thesis"),
    ),
    "stop.atr": Template(
        "stop.atr", "成本 − 倍数×ATR14",
        (_number("multiple", "ATR 倍数", default=2.0, minimum=0.1, maximum=10.0, unit="倍"),),
        severity="strong", stages=True, needs_position=True, targets=("position", "thesis"),

    "stop.ma": Template(
        "stop.ma", "跌破均线",

    ),
    "stop.breakeven": Template(
        "stop.breakeven", "浮盈超 x% 后把线抬到成本",
        (_number("trigger_pct", "触发浮盈", required=True, minimum=0.01, maximum=2.0),),
        severity="strong", stages=True, needs_position=True, targets=("position", "thesis"),

    "stop.time": Template(
        "stop.time", "持仓 N 天未达预期 → 复核",

         _number("max_gain_pct", "低于此涨幅算未达预期", default=0.0, minimum=-0.9,
                 maximum=1.0)),

    "stop.thesis_drift": Template(

        targets=("position", "thesis"), price_class=False,

               default="0.5,1.0"),),
        severity="mid", stages=True, needs_position=True, targets=("position", "thesis"),
    ),
    "price.cross_below": Template(
        "price.cross_below", "下穿指定价",

    "price.cross_above": Template(

        severity="weak", stages=False, needs_thesis=True, targets=("thesis",),
        price_class=False,

}

RULE_KINDS = tuple(TEMPLATES)


def template_for(kind: str) -> Template:
    try:
        return TEMPLATES[kind]
    except KeyError:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"未知规则类型 kind={kind!r}：只有 {', '.join(RULE_KINDS)}。"
            "名单的唯一出处是 `core/rules.py`，加一种就是在这里加一行"
        ) from None


def _as_number(param: Param, raw: object) -> float:
    try:
        value = float(raw)  # 接受 "8"，不接受 "八"：后者抛 ValueError
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise ValidationError(
            f"{param.name} 要的是数字（{param.label}），收到 {raw!r}"
        ) from None
    if not math.isfinite(value):
        # nan 与任何阈值比较都是 False ⇒ 那条规则永远不会响，而屏幕上写着「启用」
        raise ValidationError(f"{param.name} 要的是有限数（{param.label}），收到 {raw!r}")
    if param.minimum is not None and value < param.minimum:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"{param.name} 不能低于 {param.minimum:g}{param.unit}（收到 {value:g}）"
        )
    if param.maximum is not None and value > param.maximum:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"{param.name} 不能高于 {param.maximum:g}{param.unit}（收到 {value:g}）"
        )
    return value


def _as_tiers(param: Param, raw: object) -> list[float]:
    if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
        # 库里的 `params_json` 读回来就是 list[float]：`update` 改名时会把旧参数
        # 原样再校验一遍，这里必须接受自己的输出（否则改个名字都过不去）
        pieces: list[object] = list(raw)
    else:
        text = param.default if raw is None else raw
        if isinstance(text, (int, float)):
            raise ValidationError(f"{param.name} 要的是比例串（例如 0.5,1.0），收到 {raw!r}")
        pieces = [
            piece.strip()
            for piece in str(text).replace("，", ",").split(",")
            if piece.strip()
        ]
    if not pieces:
        raise ValidationError(f"{param.name} 不能是空的：至少给一档")
    steps: list[float] = []
    for piece in pieces:
        try:
            steps.append(float(piece))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ValidationError(
                f"{param.name} 里 {piece!r} 不是数字：要的是 0.5,1.0 这种比例串"
            ) from None
    if any(not math.isfinite(step) for step in steps):
        # nan/inf 过得了下面的 >0 与递增检查（比较恒为 False），然后那条规则永远不响
        raise ValidationError(f"{param.name} 的每一档都要是有限数（收到 {steps}）")
    if any(step <= 0 for step in steps):
        raise ValidationError(f"{param.name} 的每一档都要大于 0（收到 {steps}）")
    if any(b <= a for a, b in zip(steps, steps[1:])):
        raise ValidationError(
            f"{param.name} 必须递增（收到 {steps}）：1.0 那一档就是「到了目标价」，"
            "排在 0.5 前面会让第一档永远不报"
        )
    return steps


def validate_params(kind: str, raw: Mapping[str, object] | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    """补默认值 + 校形状 + 拒收野键。返回的字典可以直接进 `params_json`。

    「静默丢掉一个不认识的键」是这一层最便宜也最坏的错：用户以为写了 `percent=8`，
    库里那条规则用的还是默认的 `pct`，屏幕上两条都显示「止损比例」——没有任何一处会报错，
    直到那一次没跌破他以为的那条线。
    """
    built = template_for(kind)
    given = dict(raw or {})
    out: dict[str, object] = {}
    for param in built.params:
        if param.name not in given:
            if param.required:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"{kind} 少了必填参数 {param.name}（{param.label}）：{built.describe()}"
                )
            out[param.name] = _default_for(param)
            continue
        value = given.pop(param.name)
        if value is None and param.required:
            raise ValidationError(f"{kind} 的参数 {param.name} 是必填的（{param.label}）")
        out[param.name] = _default_for(param) if value is None else _coerce(param, value)
    if given:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"{kind} 不认这些参数：{', '.join(sorted(given))}。"
            f"这一种要的是：{built.describe()}"
        )
    return out


def _coerce(param: Param, value: object) -> object:
    if param.shape == "choice":
        text = str(value).strip()
        if text not in param.choices:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"{param.name} 只能是 {'/'.join(param.choices)}（收到 {text!r}）"
            )
        return text
    if param.shape == "tiers":
        return _as_tiers(param, value)
    return _as_number(param, value)


def _default_for(param: Param) -> object:
    return _coerce(param, param.default) if param.shape == "tiers" else param.default


def validate_filters(raw: Mapping[str, object] | None) -> dict[str, bool]:
    """四个开关全给默认值 `False`：默认不降噪，因为「一条静默的规则」比「一条吵的规则」难查。"""
    given = dict(raw or {})
    if "market_rel" in given:
        raise ValidationError(
            "过滤器 market_rel 这一层没有输入：它要比对的指数（沪深300 等）不在库里，"
            "采集链也没有指数端点。给了也不会生效，所以这里直接拒收（记 U27）。"
        )
    out: dict[str, bool] = {}
    for key in FILTER_KEYS:
        value = given.pop(key, False)
        if value is True or value is False:
            out[key] = value
        else:
            raise ValidationError(f"过滤器 {key} 要的是真或假，收到 {value!r}")
    if given:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"不认这些过滤器：{', '.join(sorted(given))}。可用的只有 {', '.join(FILTER_KEYS)}"
        )
    return out


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verdict:
    """一次判定的结果。文本与数值**同时带**：文本给人，数值给 `--json` 与复算。

    只给文本的话，机器读者只能去 parse「9.80 元」；只给数值的话，屏幕上那句话就得由
    每个显示层各自拼一遍——§5.4 规则 3（币种口径）会在某一个「各自」里丢掉。
    """

    stage: str
    value: float | None
    threshold: float | None
    value_text: str
    threshold_text: str
    #: 线是怎么算出来的那句话（折回因子、基准是哪天、缺了哪一路），进告警正文
    why: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.stage not in STAGES:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"未知阶段 stage={self.stage!r}：只有 {list(STAGES)}，"
                "冷却放行按这三个值的次序判（D94）"
            )

    @property
    def ok(self) -> bool:
        return self.value is not None and self.threshold is not None


def tier_of(score: float | None) -> int | None:
    """分项分落在第几档（0~5）。None 进 None：「没这个数」不能排进任何一档。

    §9.3 说的是「下滑超过一档」，而这里的「一档」由 `DRIFT_TIER` 定死成 20 分。
    写成档位而不是分数差，是为了让「78 → 61」这种没跨档的波动**不**报警：
    分项分本身带着窗口噪声，跨档才是能被说清的变化。
    """
    if score is None:
        return None
    return int(max(0.0, min(DRIFT_TIER * 5.0, score)) // DRIFT_TIER)


def escalates(from_stage: str | None, to_stage: str) -> bool:
    """这一次命中该不该穿过冷却（D94）。

    没有上一次 ⇒ 放行。有上一次 ⇒ 只有**更响**的那一档才放行：`pre` 报了 30 分钟内
    再来一条 `pre` 是噪音，而 `hit` 是另一件事——止损真破了。
    """
    if from_stage is None:
        return True
    try:
        return STAGE_RANK[to_stage] > STAGE_RANK[from_stage]
    except KeyError as exc:
        raise ValidationError(f"未知阶段 {exc.args[0]!r}") from None
```

  `escalates` 的异常分支在这里像过度设计（调用方给的都是 `Verdict.stage`，已经过 `__post_init__`），留着是因为它是那张表唯一的读入口：下一次有人往 `STAGES` 里加一档而忘了加 rank，红在这里比红在「止损被冷却吞了」那一天的用户屏幕上便宜。

### T-2：`RuleRepo`——写入、修订史、两道删除闸

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/storage/repos/rules.py`
- Test: `tests/storage/test_rules_repo.py`

`rules` 与 `rule_revisions` 两张表从 `0001` 就在库里（列见 `src/sams/storage/migrations/0001_init.sql:209-236`），**一个 Python 写入方都没有**。这一层的全部结构决定：

- **校验放在仓库层**（D81 的同一理由）：M3-C 之后还会有 M3-D 的派生写入方与 M4 的后台，它们都不经过服务层。挡在服务层的约束对它们是空的。
- **`rule_revisions` 只在 `update` 时写**，且写的是「改前那一份完整规则 / 改后那一份完整规则」的 JSON，不是 diff。§4.2 的「防止跌破后偷偷下移止损线」要能回答的是「当时那条线是多少」，diff 回答不了。
- **`thesis_id` 非空 ⇒ 不可删除，只能停用**（§9.2）。这不是形式主义：一条被删掉的派生止损规则，屏幕上连「它曾经存在」都查不到。
- **持仓类必须带 `account_id`**（§10.1 的去重键）。缺它的话两条不同账户的破位会共享一个 `(rule_id, market, code)` 键，A 报了 B 就被吞。`target_type="position"` 同样要带，不看这个 kind 是否读成本。
- **`target_ref` 的形状按 `target_type` 定**：`symbol` ⇒ `"A/601088"`（两半都不能空）；`thesis` ⇒ 论的 id；`watchlist`/`position` ⇒ `"*"`；`portfolio` 一律拒（U28 之前没有组合级输入）。写死成三种形状而不是「随便什么串」，是因为 `expand()` 要按它取数，而一个空 `target_ref` 会展开成零个目标——那条规则从此静默不响。`thesis` 目标即使没显式给 `thesis_id`，也要按 `target_ref` 查出那条论并把它落成 `thesis_id`（否则 `remove` 会允许删一条派生规则，绕过 §9.2；`update` 改 target 时也做同一件事）。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败用例** —— `tests/storage/test_rules_repo.py`

```python
"""`rules` 的写入契约（spec §10.1 / §9.2 / §4.2）。

`rules` 这张表从 0001 就在库里而没人写它：这一整个文件就是那个「第一次写」。
所以这里的每条断言都在钉「第一次写进来的人不许留下什么」，而不是在测一个已有实现。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from sams.core.errors import StorageError, ValidationError
from sams.storage.repos.rules import RuleRepo


@pytest.fixture
def repo(db):
    return RuleRepo(db)


def seed_user(db):
    from sams.storage.repos.users import UserRepo

    return UserRepo(db).create("trader", "correct-horse-battery").id


def add(repo, user_id, **kw):
    """一条最小可入库的规则：`kind` 与 `params` 齐备，其余走默认。"""
    params = {"kind": "stop.pct", "target_type": "position", "target_ref": "*",
              "account_id": "acct-1", "name": "止损 8%"}
    params.update(kw)
    return repo.create(user_id, **params)


def test_a_position_rule_without_an_account_is_refused(repo):
    """§10.1 的去重键含 `account_id`：缺了它，A 账户报过之后 B 账户的破位会被当重复吞掉。"""
    user = seed_user(db := repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="account_id"):
        add(repo, user, account_id=None)


def test_a_non_position_rule_may_stay_out_of_any_account(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    row = add(repo, user, kind="price.cross_below", params={"price": 10.0},
              target_type="symbol", target_ref="A/601088", account_id=None)
    assert row.account_id is None


def test_unknown_params_are_refused_before_the_insert(repo):
    """库里躺着一条「参数没人认」的规则时，屏幕上那个「止损 8%」是装饰。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="percent"):
        add(repo, user, params={"pct": 0.08, "percent": 8})


def test_defaults_are_stored_not_left_absent(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    row = add(repo, user, kind="stop.atr", params={})
    assert row.params == {"multiple": 2.0}


def test_a_thesis_rule_must_point_at_an_existing_thesis(repo):
    """外键那一层 DuckDB 不查（`0001` 里 `thesis_id` 只是列），所以「指向一条不存在的论」
    是能被写进库的：此后每次求值都对它取不到价位，屏幕上那条规则永远是「没触发」。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="观点"):
        add(repo, user, kind="thesis.odds_below", target_type="thesis",
            target_ref="does-not-exist", thesis_id="does-not-exist", account_id=None)


def test_an_empty_target_ref_is_refused(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="target_ref"):
        add(repo, user, target_type="symbol", target_ref="", account_id=None,
            kind="price.cross_below", params={"price": 1.0})


def test_a_symbol_target_ref_must_carry_the_market(repo):
    """只有代码的引用在 A 股与港股之间是同一个串（`00700`），去重键会撞。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="A/601088"):
        add(repo, user, target_type="symbol", target_ref="601088", account_id=None,
            kind="price.cross_below", params={"price": 1.0})


def test_the_template_decides_which_target_types_are_allowed(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="position"):
        add(repo, user, kind="stop.pct", target_type="watchlist", target_ref="*",
            account_id="acct-1")


def test_a_severity_outside_the_three_is_refused(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="strong"):
        add(repo, user, severity="critical")


def test_the_first_rule_for_a_user_becomes_visible_and_round_trips(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user)
    read = repo.get(made.id, user_id=user)
    assert read is not None
    assert read.name == "止损 8%"
    assert read.kind == "stop.pct"
    assert read.enabled is True
    assert read.channel_ids == []
    assert read.created_at is not None


def test_reading_another_users_rule_gives_nothing(repo):
    """仓库层的用户隔离（§4.2）：不提供「查全量再由调用方过滤」的接口。"""
    mine = seed_user(repo.db)
    theirs = repo.db.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE id <> ?", [mine]).to_dicts()[0]["id"] \
        if repo.db.query("SELECT count(*) c FROM users").to_dicts()[0]["c"] > 1 else None
    if theirs is None:
        from sams.storage.repos.users import UserRepo

        theirs = UserRepo(repo.db).create("other", "correct-horse-battery").id
    made = add(repo, mine)
    assert repo.get(made.id, user_id=theirs) is None
    assert repo.list_for(theirs) == []


def test_an_update_writes_one_revision_with_both_full_snapshots(repo):
    """§4.2：「跌破后偷偷把止损线往下挪」要能被查出来 ⇒ 改前那一份完整规则必须在库里。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user)
    after = repo.update(made.id, changed_by=user, params={"pct": 0.12})
    assert after.params == {"pct": 0.12}
    rows = repo.revisions(made.id)
    assert len(rows) == 1
    assert rows[0].before["params"] == {"pct": 0.08}
    assert rows[0].after["params"] == {"pct": 0.12}
    assert rows[0].changed_by == user


def test_an_update_that_changes_nothing_writes_no_revision(repo):
    """空修订史会让「改了几次」这个数虚高，而那一列是审计页要读的。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user)
    repo.update(made.id, changed_by=user, params={"pct": 0.08})
    assert repo.revisions(made.id) == []


def test_an_update_revalidates_the_params_of_the_whole_kind(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="pct"):
        repo.update(made.id, changed_by=user, params={})


def test_switching_the_kind_rejects_params_that_do_not_fit(repo):
    """改 kind 时旧参数不能留：`stop.pct` 的 `pct` 对 `stop.ma` 没有意义，
    留着它屏幕上那条规则会带着一个没人读的键，而 `validate_params` 本来会拒它。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="basis"):
        repo.update(made.id, changed_by=user, kind="stop.ma")


def test_a_thesis_bound_rule_cannot_be_deleted(repo):
    """§9.2：派生规则只能停用。「删掉」会把「它曾经存在」这句话一起删掉。"""
    from sams.storage.repos.theses import ThesisRepo

    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    thesis = ThesisRepo(repo.db).create(user, "A", "601088", "value")
    made = add(repo, user, kind="stop.pct", thesis_id=thesis.id, target_type="position")
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="停用"):
        repo.remove(made.id, user_id=user)
    repo.set_enabled(made.id, False, user_id=user)
    assert repo.get(made.id, user_id=user).enabled is False


def test_an_ordinary_rule_can_be_removed(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user)
    assert repo.remove(made.id, user_id=user) is True
    assert repo.get(made.id, user_id=user) is None


def test_the_default_channel_list_is_stored_as_json_and_read_back(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user, channel_ids=["c1", "c2"])
    assert repo.get(made.id, user_id=user).channel_ids == ["c1", "c2"]


def test_a_cooldown_of_zero_is_allowed_and_means_every_hit_reports(repo):
    """0 不是「没填」：填了 0 就是「每次都报」，那是有人真的要这么干时的形状。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    made = add(repo, user, cooldown_min=0)
    assert repo.get(made.id, user_id=user).cooldown_min == 0


def test_a_negative_cooldown_is_refused(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="cooldown"):
        add(repo, user, cooldown_min=-1)


def test_only_known_condition_kinds_are_written(repo):
    """`expression` 是 M5 的东西（§10.4）：现在收进来会得到一条永远不会被求值的规则。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="template"):
        add(repo, user, condition_kind="expression")


def test_list_for_orders_by_creation_and_can_filter_enabled(repo):
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    first = add(repo, user, name="一条")
    second = add(repo, user, name="两条", kind="stop.fixed",
                 params={"price": 9.0}, target_type="symbol", target_ref="A/601088",
                 account_id=None)
    repo.set_enabled(first.id, False, user_id=user)
    assert [row.id for row in repo.list_for(user)] == [first.id, second.id]
    assert [row.id for row in repo.list_for(user, enabled_only=True)] == [second.id]


def test_counts_splits_total_from_enabled(repo):
    """`on` 是 DuckDB 的保留字：别名撞上它时 `counts()` 每次都是 Parser Error，
    而仪表盘那一格只是空着——没有这条用例就没人会发现。"""
    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    stopped = add(repo, user, name="停用的")
    add(repo, user, name="在用的")
    repo.set_enabled(stopped.id, False, user_id=user)
    assert repo.counts(user) == {"total": 2, "enabled": 1}


def test_by_thesis_finds_the_derived_rules_in_creation_order(repo):
    from sams.storage.repos.theses import ThesisRepo

    user = seed_user(repo.db)
    thesis = ThesisRepo(repo.db).create(user, "A", "601088", "value")
    a = add(repo, user, name="止损", thesis_id=thesis.id)
    b = add(repo, user, name="到价", thesis_id=thesis.id, kind="price.cross_above",
            params={"price": 60.0}, target_type="thesis", target_ref=thesis.id)
    assert [row.id for row in repo.by_thesis(thesis.id)] == [a.id, b.id]
```

  `test_a_thesis_rule_must_point_at_an_existing_thesis` 里那句「观点」要求报错文案带那个词；`ThesisRepo.create(user_id, market, code, style)` 的签名以仓库里那份为准（见 `src/sams/storage/repos/theses.py:230`），**先跑一次确认它接受这四参**，不接受就读一眼签名再改用例。

- [ ] **Step 2：跑，确认红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/storage/test_rules_repo.py -q`
Expected: `ImportError: cannot import name 'RuleRepo'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/storage/repos/rules.py`

```python
"""`rules` 与 `rule_revisions`（spec §10.1、§9.2、§4.2）。

这两张表从 `0001` 就在库里而一个写入方都没有。校验因此**全放在仓库层**：
M3-D 的派生写入方与 M4 的后台都不经过 `services`，挡在服务层的约束对它们是空的（D81）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

from sams.core.clock import SYSTEM_CLOCK, Clock
from sams.core.errors import StorageError, ValidationError
from sams.core.notify import SEVERITIES
from sams.core.rules import (
    TARGET_TYPES,
    template_for,
    validate_filters,
    validate_params,
)
from sams.storage.db import Database
from sams.storage.ids import new_id

#: 只有这一种条件（§10.4：`expression` 是 M5 的事）
CONDITION_KIND = "template"

_COLUMNS = (
    "id, user_id, account_id, name, enabled, target_type, target_ref, kind,"
    " condition_kind, params_json, filters_json, cooldown_min, severity, channel_ids,"
    " thesis_id, created_at, updated_at"
)

#: 这些 target 的 `target_ref` 恒为「整个集合」，写法里带上 `*` 是为了让它可断言
#: （空串会展开成零个目标，那条规则从此静默不响，而屏幕上它看着是启用的）
_WILDCARD_TARGETS = ("watchlist", "position", "portfolio")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rule:
    id: str
    user_id: str
    name: str
    kind: str
    enabled: bool
    target_type: str
    target_ref: str
    params: dict
    filters: dict
    cooldown_min: int
    severity: str
    channel_ids: list[str]
    account_id: str | None = None
    thesis_id: str | None = None
    condition_kind: str = CONDITION_KIND
    created_at: datetime | None = None
    updated_at: datetime | None = None

    @property
    def describe(self) -> str:
        """一行说得清这条规则在等什么（屏幕上那条列表与 `--json` 读同一句）。"""
        return f"{template_for(self.kind).label}｜{self.target_type}:{self.target_ref}"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Revision:
    id: str
    rule_id: str
    changed_by: str
    before: dict
    after: dict
    changed_at: datetime | None


def _to_rule(row: Mapping[str, object]) -> Rule:
    return Rule(
        id=str(row["id"]),
        user_id=str(row["user_id"]),
        name=str(row["name"]),
        kind=str(row["kind"]),
        enabled=bool(row["enabled"]),
        target_type=str(row["target_type"]),
        target_ref=str(row["target_ref"]),
        params=json.loads(str(row["params_json"] or "{}")),
        filters=json.loads(str(row["filters_json"] or "{}")),
        cooldown_min=int(row["cooldown_min"]),
        severity=str(row["severity"]),
        channel_ids=list(json.loads(str(row["channel_ids"] or "[]"))),
        account_id=row["account_id"],
        thesis_id=row["thesis_id"],
        condition_kind=str(row["condition_kind"]),
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    )



def _snapshot(row: Rule) -> dict:
    """修订史里存的是**整份规则**，不是 diff（§4.2 要回答的是「当时那条线是多少」）。"""
    return {
        "name": row.name,
        "kind": row.kind,
        "params": row.params,
        "filters": row.filters,
        "severity": row.severity,
        "cooldown_min": row.cooldown_min,
        "channel_ids": row.channel_ids,
        "enabled": row.enabled,
        "target_type": row.target_type,
        "target_ref": row.target_ref,
        "account_id": row.account_id,
        "thesis_id": row.thesis_id,
    }


def _to_checked(row: Rule) -> dict:
    """把库里那一行还原成 `_validate` 的那个形状。

    只为了给 `update` 做一次「有没有实质变化」的比较。
    """
    return {
        "name": row.name,
        "kind": row.kind,
        "params": row.params,
        "filters": row.filters,
        "severity": row.severity,
        "cooldown_min": row.cooldown_min,
        "channel_ids": row.channel_ids,
        "target_type": row.target_type,
        "target_ref": row.target_ref,
        "account_id": row.account_id,
        "thesis_id": row.thesis_id,
    }


def _deltas(before: dict, checked: dict) -> dict:
    """两个形状相同的字典之间，哪些字段真的变了。

    只比 `_validate` 返回的那些键：`enabled` 走 `set_enabled`，时间戳每次都在动，
    把它们算进「实质变化」就等于每条规则每天都有一条修订史。
    """
    return {key: value for key, value in checked.items() if before.get(key) != value}




class RuleRepo:
    def __init__(self, db: Database, *, clock: Clock = SYSTEM_CLOCK) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.clock = clock

    # --- 写 -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def create(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        *,
        kind: str,
        name: str,
        target_type: str,
        target_ref: str,
        params: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
        filters: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
        account_id: str | None = None,
        thesis_id: str | None = None,
        severity: str | None = None,
        channel_ids: Sequence[str] = (),
        cooldown_min: int = 30,
        enabled: bool = True,
        condition_kind: str = CONDITION_KIND,
    ) -> Rule:
        checked = self._validate(
            user_id=user_id,
            name=name,
            kind=kind,
            target_type=target_type,
            target_ref=target_ref,
            params=params,
            filters=filters,
            account_id=account_id,
            thesis_id=thesis_id,
            severity=severity,
            channel_ids=channel_ids,
            cooldown_min=cooldown_min,
            condition_kind=condition_kind,
        )
        rule_id = new_id()
        now = self.clock.now_utc()
        self.db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO rules"
            " (id, user_id, account_id, name, enabled, target_type, target_ref, kind,"
            " condition_kind, params_json, filters_json, cooldown_min, severity, channel_ids,"
            " thesis_id, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            [
                rule_id,
                checked["user_id"],
                checked["account_id"],
                checked["name"],
                bool(enabled),
                checked["target_type"],
                checked["target_ref"],
                checked["kind"],
                checked["condition_kind"],
                json.dumps(checked["params"], ensure_ascii=False),
                json.dumps(checked["filters"], ensure_ascii=False),
                checked["cooldown_min"],
                checked["severity"],
                json.dumps(checked["channel_ids"], ensure_ascii=False),
                checked["thesis_id"],
                now,
                now,
            ],
        )
        created = self.get(rule_id)
        assert created is not None
        return created

    def _validate(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        name: str,
        kind: str,
        target_type: str,
        target_ref: str,
        params: Mapping[str, object] | None,
        filters: Mapping[str, object] | None,
        account_id: str | None,
        thesis_id: str | None,
        severity: str | None,
        channel_ids: Sequence[str],
        cooldown_min: int,
        condition_kind: str = CONDITION_KIND,
    ) -> dict:
        """`create` 与 `update` 共用这一套校验。

        抽出来不是为了少写几行：`update` 必须把**旧值与新值合并后**的那一份重走一遍创建时
        的全部判据，而两处各写一遍是这个文件最容易分叉的地方——分叉的后果长成「创建时不许
        的东西，改的时候改进去了」。返回校验过的同名字典，调用方只管落库。
        """
        if not user_id:
            raise ValidationError("规则必须属于一个用户：没有归属就没有路由")
        label = (name or "").strip()
        if not label:
            raise ValidationError("规则要有个名字：`sams alerts` 里那一条要说清是谁在响")
        built = template_for(kind)  # 未知 kind 在这里就拒，顺带把名单的回显给出去
        if condition_kind != CONDITION_KIND:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"condition_kind 只能是 {CONDITION_KIND!r}：受限表达式是 M5 的事（§10.4），"
                "现在收进来只会得到一条没人求值的规则"
            )
        if target_type not in TARGET_TYPES:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"未知对象类型 target_type={target_type!r}：只有 {', '.join(TARGET_TYPES)}"
            )
        if target_type == "portfolio":
            raise ValidationError(
                "组合级规则这一层还没有输入（U28）：等每日组合快照落地再接"
            )
        if target_type not in built.targets:
            allowed = "/".join(built.targets)
            raise ValidationError(
                f"{kind} 不能挂在 {target_type} 上：这一种只认 {allowed}。"
                "名单收在模板表里而不是放开全部五种，因为 `expand()` 要能对每一种给出确定的"
                "展开方式——挂错对象的规则不会报错，只会静默地不响（§1 那张表的第一行）"
            )
        ref = (target_ref or "").strip()
        self._require_target_shape(target_type, ref)
        if (built.needs_position or target_type == "position") and not account_id:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"{kind}（{built.label}）必须挂在某个账户上：去重键含 account_id，"
                "缺了它，同一只票在两个账户里的破位会互相吞（§10.1）"
            )
        if target_type == "thesis":
            # `expand()` 对 thesis 目标读的是 `thesis_id or target_ref`：这里就把它落成
            # `thesis_id`，否则一条指向论、却能被 `remove` 删掉的规则会绕过 §9.2
            thesis_id = thesis_id or ref
        if built.needs_thesis and not thesis_id:
            raise ValidationError(f"{kind} 必须挂在一条论上：它读的是那条论的价位与分项")
        if target_type == "thesis" and thesis_id is not None and ref != thesis_id:
            raise ValidationError(
                "target_type=thesis 时 target_ref 就是那条论的 id："
                "两者不一致会让 expand() 取到一条论而校验另一条"
            )
        self._require_thesis(user_id, thesis_id)
        checked_params = validate_params(kind, params)
        checked_filters = validate_filters(filters)
        level = built.severity if severity is None else severity
        if level not in SEVERITIES:
            raise ValidationError(f"未知严重级别 severity={level!r}：只有 {list(SEVERITIES)}")
        if int(cooldown_min) < 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"cooldown_min 不能为负（收到 {cooldown_min}）")
        return {
            "user_id": user_id,
            "name": label,
            "kind": kind,
            "target_type": target_type,
            "target_ref": ref,
            "params": checked_params,
            "filters": checked_filters,
            "severity": level,
            "cooldown_min": int(cooldown_min),
            "channel_ids": list(channel_ids),
            "account_id": account_id,
            "thesis_id": thesis_id,
            "condition_kind": condition_kind,
        }

    def update(
        self,
        rule_id: str,
        *,
        changed_by: str,
        user_id: str | None = None,
        name: str | None = None,
        kind: str | None = None,
        params: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
        filters: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
        severity: str | None = None,
        channel_ids: Sequence[str] | None = None,
        cooldown_min: int | None = None,
        target_type: str | None = None,
        target_ref: str | None = None,
    ) -> Rule:
        """一次改动写一条修订史（§4.2）。没有实质变化 ⇒ 不写。"""
        before = self._require(rule_id, user_id=user_id)
        patch = {
            key: value
            for key, value in {
                "name": name,
                "kind": kind,
                "params": params,
                "filters": filters,
                "severity": severity,
                "channel_ids": channel_ids,
                "cooldown_min": cooldown_min,
                "target_type": target_type,
                "target_ref": target_ref,
            }.items()
            if value is not None
        }
        if not patch:
            raise ValidationError("什么都没要改：`rule edit` 至少给一个参数")
        # 用新值重走一遍创建时的那整套校验，所以「改 kind 而旧参数不合」在这里就红
        merged = {
            "user_id": before.user_id,
            "name": patch.get("name", before.name),
            "kind": patch.get("kind", before.kind),
            "target_type": patch.get("target_type", before.target_type),
            "target_ref": patch.get("target_ref", before.target_ref),
            "params": patch.get("params", before.params),
            "filters": patch.get("filters", before.filters),
            "severity": patch.get("severity", before.severity),
            "cooldown_min": patch.get("cooldown_min", before.cooldown_min),
            "account_id": before.account_id,
            "thesis_id": before.thesis_id,
            "channel_ids": patch.get("channel_ids", before.channel_ids),
        }
        if kind is not None and kind != before.kind and params is None:
            # 换 kind 而不给新参数：旧参数对新 kind 多半是野键，让它在这里就红，
            # 而不是带着一个没人读的键进库（屏幕上那条规则于是显示着一个不生效的数）
            raise ValidationError(
                f"改成 {kind} 必须同时给新的 params：旧的那些属于 {before.kind}，"
                "留着就是屏幕上一个不生效的键"
            )
        checked = self._validate(**merged)
        if not _deltas(_to_checked(before), checked):
            # 一次「改成同样的值」不在修订史里留一行：§4.2 那张表要能回答「这条线改过几次」，
            # 把空改动算进去就是在数橡皮擦的印子
            return before
        now = self.clock.now_utc()
        with self.db.transaction():
            self.db.execute(
                "UPDATE rules SET name = ?, kind = ?, target_type = ?, target_ref = ?,"
                " params_json = ?, filters_json = ?, severity = ?, cooldown_min = ?,"
                " channel_ids = ?, thesis_id = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                [
                    checked["name"],
                    checked["kind"],
                    checked["target_type"],
                    checked["target_ref"],
                    json.dumps(checked["params"], ensure_ascii=False),
                    json.dumps(checked["filters"], ensure_ascii=False),
                    checked["severity"],
                    checked["cooldown_min"],
                    json.dumps(checked["channel_ids"], ensure_ascii=False),
                    checked["thesis_id"],
                    now,
                    before.id,
                ],
            )
            after = self.get(before.id)
            assert after is not None
            self.db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO rule_revisions (id, rule_id, changed_by, before_json, after_json,"
                " changed_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                [
                    new_id(),
                    before.id,
                    changed_by,
                    json.dumps(_snapshot(before), ensure_ascii=False),
                    json.dumps(_snapshot(after), ensure_ascii=False),
                    now,
                ],
            )
        return after

    def set_enabled(self, rule_id: str, enabled: bool, *, user_id: str | None = None) -> Rule:
        row = self._require(rule_id, user_id=user_id)
        self.db.execute(
            "UPDATE rules SET enabled = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            [bool(enabled), self.clock.now_utc(), row.id],
        )
        return self._require(rule_id, user_id=user_id)

    def remove(self, rule_id: str, *, user_id: str | None = None) -> bool:
        row = self._require(rule_id, user_id=user_id)
        if row.thesis_id:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"规则 {row.name} 是从一条观点派生出来的，不能删除，只能 `sams rule disable`："
                "「悄悄关掉止损提醒」必须留下一条能查到的记录（§9.2）"
            )
        with self.db.transaction():
            self.db.execute("DELETE FROM rule_revisions WHERE rule_id = ?", [row.id])
            self.db.execute("DELETE FROM rules WHERE id = ?", [row.id])
        return True

    # --- 读 -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get(self, rule_id: str, *, user_id: str | None = None) -> Rule | None:
        sql = f"SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM rules WHERE id = ?"
        params: list[object] = [rule_id]
        if user_id is not None:
            sql += " AND user_id = ?"
            params.append(user_id)
        rows = self.db.query(sql, params)
        return _to_rule(rows.to_dicts()[0]) if rows.height else None

    def list_for(self, user_id: str, *, enabled_only: bool = False) -> list[Rule]:
        sql = f"SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM rules WHERE user_id = ?"
        params: list[object] = [user_id]
        if enabled_only:
            sql += " AND enabled = TRUE"
        sql += " ORDER BY created_at, id"
        return [_to_rule(row) for row in self.db.query(sql, params).to_dicts()]

    def by_thesis(self, thesis_id: str) -> list[Rule]:
        rows = self.db.query(
            f"SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM rules WHERE thesis_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, id",
            [thesis_id],
        )
        return [_to_rule(row) for row in rows.to_dicts()]

    def revisions(self, rule_id: str) -> list[Revision]:
        rows = self.db.query(
            "SELECT id, rule_id, changed_by, before_json, after_json, changed_at"
            " FROM rule_revisions WHERE rule_id = ? ORDER BY changed_at, id",
            [rule_id],
        )
        return [
            Revision(
                id=str(row["id"]),
                rule_id=str(row["rule_id"]),
                changed_by=str(row["changed_by"]),
                before=json.loads(str(row["before_json"] or "{}")),
                after=json.loads(str(row["after_json"] or "{}")),
                changed_at=row["changed_at"],
            )
            for row in rows.to_dicts()
        ]

    def counts(self, user_id: str) -> dict[str, int]:
        rows = self.db.query(
            "SELECT count(*) total,"
            " coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) enabled_n"
            " FROM rules WHERE user_id = ?",
            [user_id],
        ).to_dicts()[0]
        return {"total": int(rows["total"]), "enabled": int(rows["enabled_n"])}

    # --- 内部 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _require(self, rule_id: str, *, user_id: str | None = None) -> Rule:
        row = self.get(rule_id, user_id=user_id)
        if row is None:
            tail = "" if user_id is None else "（或不属于这个用户）"
            raise StorageError(f"规则 {rule_id} 不存在{tail}")
        return row

    def _require_target_shape(self, target_type: str, target_ref: str) -> None:
        ref = (target_ref or "").strip()
        if not ref:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"target_ref 不能是空的：{target_type} 类型的规则要能展开成一个具体对象"
                "（空引用会展开成零个目标，那条规则从此静默不响）"
            )
        if target_type in _WILDCARD_TARGETS:
            if ref != "*":
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"target_type={target_type} 时 target_ref 要写 `*`（整个集合），收到 {ref!r}"
                )
            return
        if target_type == "symbol":
            market, sep, code = ref.partition("/")
            if not sep or not market or not code or "/" in code:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"target_ref 要写成 市场/代码（例如 A/601088），收到 {ref!r}："
                    "只有代码时 A 股与港股会撞同一个键"
                )

    def _require_thesis(self, user_id: str, thesis_id: str | None) -> None:
        if thesis_id is None:
            return
        from sams.storage.repos.theses import ThesisRepo

        if ThesisRepo(self.db).get(user_id, thesis_id) is None:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"观点 {thesis_id} 不存在（或不属于这个用户）：一条读不到价位的规则"
                "会永远显示成「没触发」"
            )
```

### T-3：`AlertRepo.latest_for`——去重键上最近的那一次命中

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/sams/storage/repos/alerts.py`（在 `list_for` 之后加一个方法）
- Test: `tests/storage/test_alerts_repo.py`（追加用例，不新建文件；已有 `db` / `repo` / `seeded_user` 夹具与 `CREATE_KW`）

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试**（追加到 `tests/storage/test_alerts_repo.py` 末尾）

```python
# --- 冷却与去重的查询（§10.1 的键 / D94 的上一档）------------------------------------------


def seed_hit(
    repo, seeded_user, *, minutes_ago=0, rule="r1", code="601088", account="acc1", stage="pre"
):
    """按「距现在多少分钟」种一条告警：断言里不写日期，跑在什么时候都一样。"""
    from datetime import timedelta

    from sams.core.clock import SYSTEM_CLOCK

    moment = SYSTEM_CLOCK.now_utc() - timedelta(minutes=minutes_ago)
    return repo.create(
        seeded_user,
        **{
            **CREATE_KW,
            "rule_id": rule,
            "account_id": account,
            "code": code,
            "stage": stage,
            "triggered_at": moment,
        },
    )


def test_latest_for_picks_the_newest_row_on_the_dedupe_key(repo, seeded_user):
    older = seed_hit(repo, seeded_user, minutes_ago=120, stage="pre")
    newer = seed_hit(repo, seeded_user, minutes_ago=10, stage="hit")
    seed_hit(repo, seeded_user, minutes_ago=5, rule="r2")  # 别的规则，不算这条的上一档

    got = repo.latest_for("r1", "A", "601088", account_id="acc1")

    assert got is not None
    assert got.id == newer.id, f"该拿最近那一条，拿到了 {older.id}"
    assert got.stage == "hit"


def test_latest_for_does_not_let_another_accounts_hit_silence_this_one(repo, seeded_user):
    """§1 那张表的第三行：同一只票两个账户，A 的破位不能把 B 的吃掉。"""
    seed_hit(repo, seeded_user, account="acc-a", minutes_ago=5, stage="hit")

    assert repo.latest_for("r1", "A", "601088", account_id="acc-b") is None
    assert repo.latest_for("r1", "A", "601088", account_id="acc-a") is not None


def test_latest_for_matches_a_missing_account(repo, seeded_user):
    """非持仓类规则的 `account_id` 是 NULL，而 SQL 里 `NULL = NULL` 不成立。

    这一枚钉的是 DuckDB 那一侧：写成 `account_id = ?` 时这类规则永远查不到自己的上一次
    命中，于是每一趟都「穿过冷却」——症状是同一条止损每跑一次重发一遍，而库里全是它
    自己刚写的那些行。
    """
    seed_hit(repo, seeded_user, account=None, minutes_ago=30, stage="pre")

    assert repo.latest_for("r1", "A", "601088") is not None
    assert repo.latest_for("r1", "A", "601088", account_id=None) is not None


def test_latest_for_is_empty_when_nothing_was_raised(repo, seeded_user):
    assert repo.latest_for("r1", "A", "000001") is None
```

Expected: `AttributeError: 'AlertRepo' object has no attribute 'latest_for'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现**（`alerts.py`，放在 `list_for` 与 `record` 之间）

```python
    def latest_for(
        self,
        rule_id: str,
        market: str,
        code: str,
        *,
        account_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> Alert | None:
        """这条去重键上最近的一次命中（§10.1 的键 + D94 的判据都从它取）。

        问「最近一条」而不是「冷却窗口内有没有」：引擎要知道的是**上一档是什么**，
        而 `EXISTS` 只回答有没有——「有没有」决定不了要不要穿过冷却。一次问清还省掉
        第二次查询；这一层是单进程同步一趟，没有并发插入要担心。

        `account_id` 参与匹配，且**不是**「给了才过滤」：`IS NOT DISTINCT FROM` 让
        NULL 与 NULL 算同一件事。写成 `= ?` 的话，所有非持仓类规则（`account_id` 为
        NULL）永远查不到自己的上一次命中，于是每一趟都放行。
        走的正是 `idx_alerts_throttle (rule_id, market, code, triggered_at)`。
        """
        rows = self.db.query(
            f"SELECT {_ALERT_COLUMNS} FROM alerts"
            " WHERE rule_id = ? AND market = ? AND code = ?"
            " AND account_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM ?"
            " ORDER BY triggered_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
            [rule_id, market, code, account_id],
        )
        return self._to_alert(rows.to_dicts()[0]) if rows.height else None
```

### T-4：迁移 `0016`——周复检的那条基线要有地方住

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/storage/migrations/0016_rule_baseline.sql`
- Modify: `src/sams/storage/repos/theses.py`（`_COLUMNS`、`Thesis`、`_to_theses`、新方法 `set_baseline`）
- Test: `tests/test_migration_0016.py`（新建）+ `tests/storage/test_theses_repo.py`（追加）

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/test_migration_0016.py`

```python
"""0016：`theses` 上的三项基线（spec §9.3 / D96）。

形状照 `tests/storage/test_migration_0015.py` 那份断言：逐列、可空、旧行读回来是 None。
这条迁移只加列，不动任何已有值。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from sams.storage.db import Database
from sams.storage.migrate import apply_migrations


def fresh(tmp_path) -> Database:
    """迁移文件必须在第一次构建库之前就存在于磁盘：apply 时才读目录算校验和。"""
    db = Database.open(str(tmp_path / "x.duckdb"))
    apply_migrations(db)
    return db


def test_theses_gains_the_three_baseline_columns(tmp_path):
    db = fresh(tmp_path)
    assert {"baseline_score", "baseline_items", "baseline_on"} <= db.columns("theses")
    row = db.query("SELECT baseline_score, baseline_items, baseline_on FROM theses LIMIT 0")
    assert row.columns == ["baseline_score", "baseline_items", "baseline_on"]


def test_applying_migrations_twice_adds_nothing(tmp_path):
    """加一条迁移不能弄脏校验和：`sams db migrate` 在真库上重跑必须是幂等 no-op。"""
    db = fresh(tmp_path)
    before = db.query("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM schema_migrations").to_dicts()[0]["n"]
    apply_migrations(db)
    after = db.query("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM schema_migrations").to_dicts()[0]["n"]
    assert before == after == 16


def test_an_old_row_reads_back_with_no_baseline(tmp_path):
    """0013 之前建的那条论没有基线，读回来必须是 None 而不是 0 分。

    0 分是「这项最差」，None 是「没比过」——把后者读成前者，`thesis.drift` 会在一条
    从没复检过的论上永远报「掉到底了」。
    """
    db = fresh(tmp_path)
    db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO theses (id, user_id, market, code, style, status) VALUES"
        " ('t1', 'u1', 'A', '601088', 'value', 'draft')"
    )
    row = db.query("SELECT baseline_score, baseline_items, baseline_on FROM theses").to_dicts()[0]
    assert row["baseline_score"] is None
    assert row["baseline_items"] is None
    assert row["baseline_on"] is None
```

- [ ] **Step 3：写迁移** —— `src/sams/storage/migrations/0016_rule_baseline.sql`

```sql
-- 0016：周复检要的那条「基线」（spec §9.3 / D96）
--
-- §9.3 的原话是「分项分较建仓时下滑超过一档」，而「建仓时」是一个必须被记下来的事实。
-- 库里没有它，`thesis.drift` 只剩两种错法：每次都拿今天的分和今天的分比（恒不漂移），
-- 或者拿第一次跑到那条规则时的分当基线（跑得晚一天，基线就换了，而屏幕上看不出来）。
--
-- M2-D 当时特意不给 thesis 存估值快照（D76：估值可重算，存一份就是第二个真相）。
-- 这一条不是推翻它：可重算的前提是**输入还在**，而 financials / valuation_daily 会随新的
-- 披露改写，`composite.score` 因此不是一个可重算的历史事实。这里存的是**当时那一个数**
-- 和当时那几路分项，不是那一次的输入。
--
-- 三列而不是三张表：一条论只有一条现行基线，改基线等于重新建仓（那是另一条论的事）。
-- `baseline_items` 存 JSON 是为了让「掉的是哪一档」能被指出来——只存总分回答不了那句话。
-- DuckDB 的 `ADD COLUMN` 不能带 NOT NULL（实测，0015 的头注是同一条），所以三列都可空，
-- 「没有基线」由读取侧显式判断（`services/rules/recheck.py` 里 D96 的那句谎）。
ALTER TABLE theses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS baseline_score DOUBLE;
ALTER TABLE theses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS baseline_items VARCHAR;
ALTER TABLE theses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS baseline_on DATE;
```

- [ ] **Step 4：仓库层把它读回来** —— `src/sams/storage/repos/theses.py`

顶部 import 加两样（现在都没有）：`import json`、`from collections.abc import Mapping`。

`_COLUMNS` 变成：

```python
_COLUMNS = (
    "id, user_id, market, code, style, status, entry_low, entry_high, stop_price, "
    "target_price, reward_risk, win_rate, win_rate_samples, levels_on, created_at, updated_at, "
    "archived_at, baseline_score, baseline_items, baseline_on"

```

`Thesis` 在 `archived_at` 之前插三个带默认值的字段（`levels_on` 之后）：

```python
    #: 周复检的基线（0016 / D96）：建仓时那一个总分、那几路分项、属于哪一天。
    #: 三者同进同出，所以 `recheck` 只判 `baseline_score is None`。
    baseline_score: float | None = None
    baseline_items: tuple[tuple[str, float], ...] = ()
    baseline_on: date | None = None
```

`_to_theses` 里跟着加。分项存的是 JSON 对象，读成**有序元组**——「哪一路掉的」要说得出名字，而 `dict` 的顺序在断言里不可读：

```python
            archived_at=r["archived_at"],
            baseline_score=r["baseline_score"],
            baseline_items=tuple(
                sorted(
                    (str(key), float(value))
                    for key, value in json.loads(r["baseline_items"] or "{}").items()
                )
            ),
            baseline_on=r["baseline_on"],
```

新方法，放在 `set_levels` 之后（挨着它是因为两者都是「不动状态、不写事件的一次写入」）。先加一个共用的分数闸（放在 `_check_levels` 附近）：

```python
def _score(label: str, value: object) -> float:
    """0~100 的分项分：`True` 与 `"50"` 不是数，NaN 写进 JSON 之后连读都读不回来。"""
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} 要是一个数（收到 {value!r}）")
    number = float(value)
    if not isfinite(number) or not 0.0 <= number <= 100.0:
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} 要在 0~100（收到 {value}）：分项分就是这个刻度")
    return number
```

```python
    def set_baseline(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        thesis_id: str,
        *,
        score: float | None,
        items: Mapping[str, float],
        on: date,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> Thesis:
        """写下复检的基准（0016）。写一次就不再改：已经有基线的那条直接拒。

        `score=None` 会被拒：那不是「0 分」而是「这一趟没算出分」，把它写成基线等于给一条
        论钉死一个永远达不到的基准——此后每趟掉档都是它的错。
        归档那条也拒，理由与 `set_levels` 同一条：`archived` 是终点，改它是在重写历史。
        """
        if score is None:
            raise ValidationError("基线分数不能是空的：那是「没比过」，不是「最差」")
        number = _score("基线分数", score)
        current = self.require(user_id, thesis_id)
        if current.status == ARCHIVED:
            raise StorageError(
                f"{thesis_id} 已经归档：基线是「建仓时那一眼」，归档的论不再有那一眼"
            )
        if current.baseline_score is not None:
            raise StorageError(
                f"{thesis_id} 已经有基线（{current.baseline_on}）：改基线等于改历史，"
                "要换基准就另开一条论"
            )
        cleaned = {str(key): _score(f"分项 {key}", value) for key, value in items.items()}
        self.db.execute(
            "UPDATE theses SET baseline_score = ?, baseline_items = ?, baseline_on = ?,"
            " updated_at = ? WHERE user_id = ? AND id = ? AND baseline_score IS NULL",
            [
                number,
                json.dumps(cleaned, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True),
                on,
                now,
                current.user_id,
                current.id,
            ],
        )
        found = self.get(user_id, thesis_id)
        assert found is not None  # 同一个库里刚 UPDATE 过
        return found
```

```python
# --- 复检基线（0016 / D96）-----------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_baseline_round_trips_score_items_and_day(db):
    thesis = make(db)

    got = ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
        USER,
        thesis.id,
        score=72.5,
        items={"pr": 80.0, "percentile": 61.0},
        on=date(2024, 7, 1),
        now=T0,
    )

    assert got.baseline_score == 72.5
    assert got.baseline_on == date(2024, 7, 1)
    assert got.baseline_items == (("percentile", 61.0), ("pr", 80.0))


def test_a_thesis_without_a_baseline_reads_back_as_never_compared(db):
    """None 不是 0：D96 那句「从没比过 ≠ 没漂移」在仓库层的钉子。"""
    thesis = make(db)

    assert thesis.baseline_score is None
    assert thesis.baseline_items == ()
    assert thesis.baseline_on is None


def test_an_empty_baseline_score_is_refused(db):
    thesis = make(db)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不能是空的"):
        ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
            USER, thesis.id, score=None, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
        )


def test_a_baseline_outside_the_item_scale_is_refused(db):
    """分项分是 0~100 的刻度：7200 分那种数进来，`tier_of` 会把它按 100 处理而看不出不对。"""
    thesis = make(db)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="0~100"):
        ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
            USER, thesis.id, score=7200.0, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
        )


def test_setting_the_baseline_does_not_write_an_event(db):
    """价位与基线都不是状态跳转：`thesis_events` 里不该因此多出一跳。"""
    repo = ThesisRepo(db)
    thesis = make(db)

    got = repo.set_baseline(
        USER, thesis.id, score=50.0, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
    )

    assert got.status == thesis.status
    assert len(repo.events(USER, thesis.id)) == 1  # 只有开局那一跳


def test_setting_the_baseline_twice_is_refused(db):
    """基线是「建仓时那一眼」：悄悄改掉它，`thesis.drift` 就再也说不清掉的是哪一档。"""
    repo = ThesisRepo(db)
    thesis = make(db)
    repo.set_baseline(USER, thesis.id, score=50.0, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0)

    with pytest.raises(StorageError, match="已经有基线"):
        repo.set_baseline(USER, thesis.id, score=60.0, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 2), now=T1)


def test_a_baseline_for_an_archived_thesis_is_refused(db):
    repo = ThesisRepo(db)
    thesis = make(db)
    thesis = walk(
        repo, thesis, PROPOSED, OPENED, TRACKING, HIT_TARGET, ARCHIVED,
        entry_low=30.0, entry_high=32.0, stop_price=28.0, target_price=40.0, reward_risk=2.0,
    )

    with pytest.raises(StorageError, match="已经归档"):
        repo.set_baseline(USER, thesis.id, score=50.0, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T1)


def test_a_baseline_requires_a_number_not_a_bool_or_string(db):
    thesis = make(db)

    for bad in (True, "50"):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="一个数"):
            ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
                USER, thesis.id, score=bad, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
            )


def test_a_non_finite_baseline_score_is_refused(db):
    thesis = make(db)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="0~100"):
        ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
            USER, thesis.id, score=float("nan"), items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("items", [{"pr": float("nan")}, {"pr": 7200.0}, {"pr": True}])
def test_baseline_items_are_held_to_the_same_scale(db, items):
    """分项分要能进 JSON 又读得回来：NaN 连 JSON 都不是，7200 会被 `tier_of` 按 100 处理。"""
    thesis = make(db)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="pr"):
        ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
            USER, thesis.id, score=50.0, items=items, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
        )


def test_a_baseline_cannot_be_written_for_another_users_thesis(db):
    thesis = make(db)

    with pytest.raises(StorageError, match="不存在"):
        ThesisRepo(db).set_baseline(
            OTHER, thesis.id, score=50.0, items={}, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
        )
```

### T-5：`services/_fold.py`——折回现价口径只写一遍

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/_fold.py`
- Modify: `src/sams/services/valuation.py:405-457`（删掉那七个私有函数，改成 import）
- Test: `tests/services/test_fold.py`（新建）

规则引擎要复现估值层的同一件外套（`stop.atr` 拿到的 ATR 必须是现价口径，否则除权日会误报破位，§10.2 的强制验收用例），而复制 `valuation.py` 那七个私有函数就是第二个真相。这一刀先于引擎做，因为它改的是已经绿着的用例里的代码。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_fold.py`

```python
"""折回现价口径：`现价 ÷ 当日后复权收盘` 这个比值，以及用它搬的那几路。

算式与 `valuation.py` 里原来那七个私有函数逐字一致——这一层的验收标准是「行为没变」，
所以断言写的都是「同一份输入得到同一个数」，不是「看起来合理」。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import polars as pl
import pytest

from sams.services._fold import (
    before_last,
    fold,
    fold_to_price_basis,
    last,
    pct_change,
    weekly_line,
    window_high,
)


def test_fold_is_price_over_hfq_close() -> None:
    assert fold(11.0, 100.0) == 0.11


def test_fold_gives_up_when_either_side_is_missing() -> None:
    """None、0、负数都返回 None：合成侧自己会拒，这里不能伪造一个 0 因子。"""
    assert fold(None, 100.0) is None
    assert fold(11.0, None) is None
    assert fold(11.0, 0.0) is None
    assert fold(0.0, 100.0) is None
    assert fold(-1.0, 100.0) is None
    assert fold(11.0, -100.0) is None


def test_folding_a_value_moves_it_by_the_factor() -> None:
    assert fold_to_price_basis(9.0, 0.5) == 4.5
    assert fold_to_price_basis(None, 0.5) is None
    assert fold_to_price_basis(9.0, None) is None


def test_last_is_the_tail_of_a_column_or_none() -> None:
    table = pl.DataFrame({"close": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]})
    assert last(table, "close") == 3.0
    assert last(table, "nope") is None
    assert last(pl.DataFrame({"close": []}), "close") is None


def test_before_last_is_the_one_before_the_tail_or_none() -> None:
    """上一根取 `[-2]`：两行表上 `[-2]` 与 `[0]` 恰好同值，三行才钉得住下标。"""
    table = pl.DataFrame({"close": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]})
    assert before_last(table, "close") == 2.0
    assert before_last(table, "nope") is None
    assert before_last(pl.DataFrame({"close": [1.0]}), "close") is None
    assert before_last(pl.DataFrame({"close": []}), "close") is None


def test_window_high_walks_the_adjusted_column() -> None:
    """用 `close_hfq` 而不是裸价：跨除权日时裸价那个「下跌」是分红，不是有人付过更低的价。"""
    table = pl.DataFrame({"close_hfq": [1.0, 5.0, 2.0, 4.0]})
    assert window_high(table, 3) == 5.0
    assert window_high(table, 0) is None
    assert window_high(pl.DataFrame({"close": [1.0]}), 3) is None
    assert window_high(pl.DataFrame({"close_hfq": []}), 3) is None


def test_pct_change_is_the_ratio_minus_one_on_the_adjusted_series() -> None:
    table = pl.DataFrame({"close_hfq": [10.0, 11.0]})
    # 11/10 - 1 的浮点结果是 0.10000000000000009，算式逐字保留，断言按近似写
    assert pct_change(table, "close_hfq") == pytest.approx(0.1)
    assert pct_change(pl.DataFrame({"close_hfq": [10.0]}), "close_hfq") is None
    assert pct_change(table, "nope") is None
    assert pct_change(pl.DataFrame({"close_hfq": [0.0, 11.0]}), "close_hfq") is None
    assert pct_change(pl.DataFrame({"close_hfq": [-10.0, 11.0]}), "close_hfq") is None


def test_weekly_line_folds_with_its_own_series() -> None:
    """周线用它自己的当日因子：两条序列的后复权锚可能不在同一天（模块开头那条理由）。"""
    weekly = pl.DataFrame({"ma20": [120.0], "close": [110.0], "close_hfq": [100.0]})
    assert weekly_line(weekly) == 132.0  # 120 × (110 / 100)
    assert weekly_line(pl.DataFrame({"ma20": [120.0], "close": [110.0]})) is None
    assert weekly_line(pl.DataFrame()) is None
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_fold.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services._fold'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/_fold.py`

```python
"""把后复权口径的数搬回现价口径（spec §5.5 / D73）。

库里的指标都是**在后复权序列上**算的（跨除权日才可比），而用户看的是现价、交易的是现价，
阈值也是按现价填的。所以每一路指标出门前都要乘一个 `现价 ÷ 当日后复权收盘`。

这个文件存在的理由是「同一件外套不能有两件」：`services/valuation.py` 用它合成结论，
`services/rules/` 用它判止损。两边各写一遍的后果是除权日只有一侧折回，于是那条止损在
分红当天报「已跌破」——§10.2 把它列为强制验收用例，就是这个原因。

因子必须来自**它自己那条序列**（周线用周线的当日收盘与当日后复权收盘）：后复权的绝对
水平跟着读取窗口的锚走，两条序列的锚可能不在同一天，而比值在同一序列内与锚无关。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import polars as pl


def fold(price: float | None, hfq_last: float | None) -> float | None:
    """后复权 → 现价的换算系数。两个数齐了才有它，否则 None（合成侧自己会拒）。"""
    if price is None or hfq_last is None or hfq_last <= 0 or price <= 0:
        return None
    return price / hfq_last


def fold_to_price_basis(value: float | None, factor: float | None) -> float | None:
    if value is None or factor is None:
        return None
    return value * factor


def last(table: pl.DataFrame, column: str) -> Any:
    if table.height == 0 or column not in table.columns:
        return None
    return table[column][-1]


def before_last(table: pl.DataFrame, column: str) -> Any:
    if table.height < 2 or column not in table.columns:
        return None
    return table[column][-2]


def window_high(table: pl.DataFrame, bars: int) -> float | None:
    """窗口内最高的那根后复权收盘（**还没**折回现价口径：调用方拿自己那条序列的因子折它）。

    用后复权而不是裸价：跨除权日时裸价那个「下跌」是分红，不是有人付过更低的价。
    """
    if table.height == 0 or bars <= 0 or "close_hfq" not in table.columns:
        return None
    return table["close_hfq"].tail(bars).max()


def pct_change(table: pl.DataFrame, column: str) -> float | None:
    """最后一根相对上一根的涨跌幅（在后复权口径里算：跨除权日时裸价那个差是分红）。"""
    now, prior = last(table, column), before_last(table, column)
    if now is None or prior is None or prior <= 0:
        return None
    return now / prior - 1.0


def weekly_line(weekly_table: pl.DataFrame) -> float | None:
    """周 MA20 折回现价口径：用它**自己那条序列**的当日因子，不借日线的。"""
    return fold_to_price_basis(
        last(weekly_table, "ma20"),
        fold(last(weekly_table, "close"), last(weekly_table, "close_hfq")),
    )
```

### T-6：`services/rules/snapshot.py`——唯一取数处

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/__init__.py`
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/snapshot.py`
