### T-15：`derive.py`——§9.2 的六条派生规则与「改参数回写哪一列」

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/derive.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_derive.py`

**§9.2 那六行落到这一层时要做掉的四个决定，先写清：**

1. **「达到目标价」那一行写的 kind 是 `price.cross_above`，实现取 `tp.tiered`。** 那一行的
   说明列写的是「分批止盈提醒」，而 §10.2 里唯一能报「到了半程」与「到了目标」两档的就是
   `tp.tiered`；用 `price.cross_above` 就得把 `target_price` 抄进 `params`，抄了就要在两处同步，
   而 §9.2 只规定了一个同步方向（规则 → 论）。`tp.tiered` 走 `Ask.thesis` 读那一列，不抄数。
2. **只有两条派生规则抄了论上的数字**（`stop.fixed` 抄 `stop_price`、`price.cross_below` 抄
   `entry_high`），所以回写表只有两项。剩下四条读的是论本身或日历，改了论不必动它们。
3. **回写只发生在「挂着 `thesis_id`」的规则上。** 用户自己建的一条 `price.cross_below` 里那个
   数是他心里的价，不是他论点上的区间上沿——回写会把一条与当前论点无关的东西写进论点。
   这一条与 `RuleRepo.remove` 的「`thesis_id` 非空不可删除」是同一个判据的两个方向。
4. **`tiers` 进 `params` 时可以用比例串（`"0.5,1.0"`），也可以用校验后的列表。** 模板表把
   这一族的形状定成 `shape="tiers"`；`_as_tiers` 解析串、原样接受 `list[float]`（裸数字仍然
   拒收）。库里的 `params_json` 存的是 `list[float]`——写进去用串、读出来是列表，而 update
   会把读回来的那份再校验一遍，两种形状必须都吃得下。这一条在 Step 1 的用例里两边各测一次。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_derive.py`

```python
"""§9.2：一条论 → 六条规则。派生要能重复跑，回写要能指名道姓。

仓库层全用真的：`by_thesis` 与 `remove` 那两道闸就是这一枚文件的验收线，
把它们 stub 掉等于把「不可删除，只能停用」那句话删掉。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import fields
from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.core.rules import template_for
from sams.services.rules.derive import (
    TIERS,
    WRITE_BACK,
    NotDerived,
    RuleDeriver,
    blueprints_for,
    write_back,
)
from sams.storage.repos.rules import RuleRepo
from sams.storage.repos.theses import Thesis, ThesisRepo

USER = "u-derive"
T0 = datetime(2026, 10, 20, 1, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
LEVELS = {
    "entry_low": 32.0,
    "entry_high": 36.0,
    "stop_price": 28.0,
    "target_price": 52.0,
    "reward_risk": 2.0,
}
SIX = {
    "price.cross_below",
    "stop.fixed",
    "tp.tiered",
    "thesis.odds_below",
    "scheduled.reevaluate",
    "thesis.drift",
}
#: 与账户无关的那五条：论的事实，换个账户还是同一件事
FIVE_NOT_PER_ACCOUNT = SIX - {"tp.tiered"}


@pytest.fixture
def theses(db) -> ThesisRepo:
    return ThesisRepo(db)


@pytest.fixture
def rules(db) -> RuleRepo:
    return RuleRepo(db)


def make_thesis(theses, **over):
    levels = dict(LEVELS)
    levels.update(over)
    return theses.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="601088",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status="proposed",
        **{k: v for k, v in levels.items() if v is not None},
    )


def skip_reasons(got):
    """这次没建的那些，连它们各自的理由。"""
    return {item.kind: item.reason for item in got if isinstance(item, NotDerived)}


def one(rows, kind):
    """按 kind 取那一条：不许靠 `created[0]`——建表顺序变了不该让用例变色。"""
    return next(row for row in rows if row.kind == kind)


def test_six_blueprints_when_the_thesis_is_complete(theses) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    got = blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")
    assert len(got) == 6
    assert {item.kind for item in got} == SIX
    assert skip_reasons(got) == {}


def test_every_blueprint_names_which_column_it_came_from(theses) -> None:
    """`reason` 里那句「凭什么建这条」上的数字不许是常量的回声。

    1.5 是 §9.2 那一行写死的，5 是 `core/rules.py` 里 `within_days` 的默认：断字面值，
    改常量必须让用例变红——那两个数是给用户看的承诺，不是可以自由漂移的实现细节。
    """
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    by_kind = {item.kind: item for item in blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")}
    assert "36" in by_kind["price.cross_below"].reason
    assert "28" in by_kind["stop.fixed"].reason
    assert "52" in by_kind["tp.tiered"].reason
    assert by_kind["tp.tiered"].params["tiers"] == TIERS
    assert by_kind["thesis.odds_below"].params["threshold"] == 1.5
    assert "跌破 1.5" in by_kind["thesis.odds_below"].reason
    assert by_kind["scheduled.reevaluate"].params["within_days"] == 5


def test_the_two_copied_prices_are_the_thesis_upper_edge_and_the_stop(theses) -> None:
    """蓝图里那两个数是**从哪一列抄的**：抄错列，屏幕上那句话还说得漂漂亮亮，
    而回写会把目标价写进论的 `stop_price`（`write_back` 按 kind 取列，不看 `reason`）。
    """
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    by_kind = {item.kind: item for item in blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")}
    assert by_kind["price.cross_below"].params["price"] == 36.0  # 区间上沿
    assert by_kind["stop.fixed"].params["price"] == 28.0  # 止损价


def test_a_missing_target_skips_the_tier_only(theses) -> None:
    """缺哪一列就少哪一条，并且说得出为什么——不是六条悄悄变成五条。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses, target_price=None)
    got = blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")
    missing = skip_reasons(got)
    assert set(missing) == {"tp.tiered"}
    assert "目标价" in missing["tp.tiered"]
    assert len(got) == 6


def test_a_tier_without_an_account_is_skipped_with_a_named_reason(theses) -> None:
    """`tp.tiered` 读的是这笔的成本：没有账户就没有成本，硬建出来是一条永远不响的规则。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    missing = skip_reasons(blueprints_for(thesis))
    assert "账户" in missing["tp.tiered"]


def test_the_stored_rules_carry_the_thesis_numbers(theses, rules) -> None:
    """入库那一份也得是那两个数：蓝图对、`create` 走形（丢键、串化）一样是错的规则。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    stored = rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)
    assert one(stored, "price.cross_below").params["price"] == 36.0
    assert one(stored, "stop.fixed").params["price"] == 28.0


def test_the_reevaluate_rule_is_derived_with_the_open_alert_filter(
    theses, rules
) -> None:
    """`templates.scheduled_reevaluate` 明写「T-15 派生这一条时默认带 `no_open_alert`」：
    披露日窗口内每个交易日都会各判中一次，不带这个开关就是那段窗口天天响（§10.1 降噪）。
    """
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    reeval = one(blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1"), "scheduled.reevaluate")
    assert reeval.filters == {"no_open_alert": True}
    RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    stored = rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)
    assert one(stored, "scheduled.reevaluate").filters["no_open_alert"] is True
    # 另外五条没带这个开关：§10.1 的取向是「默认不降噪，静默的规则比吵的规则难查」
    assert {
        row.filters["no_open_alert"]
        for row in stored
        if row.kind != "scheduled.reevaluate"
    } == {False}


def test_derive_creates_six_and_attaches_them_to_the_thesis(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    report = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    assert len(report.created) == 6 and report.skipped == ()
    stored = rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)
    assert len(stored) == 6
    assert {row.target_type for row in stored} == {"thesis"}
    # 只有 `tp.tiered` 真的与账户有关：另外五条是论的事实，挂上账户就等于让引擎的去重键
    # （§10.1 含 account_id）带一个与这条规则无关的账户
    assert one(stored, "tp.tiered").account_id == "acc-1"
    assert {
        row.account_id for row in stored if row.kind in FIVE_NOT_PER_ACCOUNT
    } == {None}
    # 写进去的是比例串，库里读出来的是校验过的列表（`tp.tiered` 求值时按这个形状取）
    assert one(stored, "tp.tiered").params["tiers"] == [0.5, 1.0]


def test_deriving_twice_creates_nothing(rules, theses) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    deriver = RuleDeriver(rules, theses)
    deriver.derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    again = deriver.derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    assert again.created == ()
    assert set(again.already) == SIX
    assert len(again.already) == 6
    stored = rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)
    assert len(stored) == 6
    assert sum(1 for row in stored if row.kind == "tp.tiered") == 1


def test_a_second_account_adds_only_its_own_tier(rules, theses) -> None:
    """同一论在第二个账户上建仓：只有分批止盈要再建一条（那一档读的是这一笔的成本），
    另外五条不许按账户复制——复制就是同一件事发 N 条重复告警。
    """
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    deriver = RuleDeriver(rules, theses)
    deriver.derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    again = deriver.derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-2")
    assert [row.kind for row in again.created] == ["tp.tiered"]
    assert [row.account_id for row in again.created] == ["acc-2"]
    assert set(again.already) == FIVE_NOT_PER_ACCOUNT
    stored = rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)
    assert len(stored) == 7
    assert {
        row.account_id for row in stored if row.kind == "tp.tiered"
    } == {"acc-1", "acc-2"}
    assert {
        row.account_id for row in stored if row.kind in FIVE_NOT_PER_ACCOUNT
    } == {None}


def test_a_second_derive_does_not_reset_edited_params(rules, theses) -> None:
    """幂等是「跳过」不是「覆盖」：用户把止损线改成 30 后再派生，库里得留着他那个 30。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = one(made, "stop.fixed")
    rules.update(stop.id, changed_by=USER, params={"price": 30.0})
    again = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id)
    assert again.created == ()
    kept = one(rules.by_thesis(thesis.id), "stop.fixed")
    assert kept.params["price"] == 30.0


def test_a_tier_list_survives_a_second_validation(rules, theses) -> None:
    """`tiers` 的两种形状都要在这一层走得通：库里读回的是列表，而 `update` 会把这一份
    再校验一遍——只测串那一支的话，「列表进得去」这件事其实没人保证过。
    """
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    tier = one(
        RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1").created,
        "tp.tiered",
    )
    assert tier.params["tiers"] == [0.5, 1.0]
    updated = rules.update(tier.id, changed_by=USER, params={"tiers": [0.3, 0.9]})
    assert updated.params["tiers"] == [0.3, 0.9]
    assert one(rules.by_thesis(thesis.id), "tp.tiered").params["tiers"] == [0.3, 0.9]


def test_deriving_an_empty_thesis_creates_only_the_two_that_need_no_level(
    theses, rules
) -> None:
    """一条只有方向和风格的论：价位类全跳过，日历与漂移那两条照样建。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(
        theses,
        entry_low=None,
        entry_high=None,
        stop_price=None,
        target_price=None,
        reward_risk=None,
    )
    report = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id)
    assert {row.kind for row in report.created} == {
        "scheduled.reevaluate",
        "thesis.drift",
    }
    assert len(report.skipped) == 4


def test_a_derived_rule_cannot_be_removed_but_can_be_disabled(theses, rules) -> None:
    """§9.2 那句「不可删除，只能停用」：派生出来的止损提醒不能被一次 `rule remove` 悄悄关掉。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = one(made, "stop.fixed")
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不能删除"):
        rules.remove(stop.id, user_id=USER)
    assert rules.set_enabled(stop.id, False, user_id=USER).enabled is False


# --- 改参数 → 回写论上那一列 -------------------------------------------------------------


def test_editing_a_derived_stop_moves_the_thesis_stop(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = one(made, "stop.fixed")
    assert write_back(stop, {"price": 28.0}, {"price": 30.0}) == {"stop_price": 30.0}


def test_editing_a_derived_entry_band_moves_the_upper_edge(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    cross = one(made, "price.cross_below")
    assert write_back(cross, {"price": 36.0}, {"price": 34.0}) == {"entry_high": 34.0}


def test_an_unchanged_number_writes_nothing(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = one(made, "stop.fixed")
    assert write_back(stop, {"price": 28.0}, {"price": 28.0}) == {}


def test_a_change_without_a_price_writes_back_nothing(theses, rules) -> None:
    """改的是别的键（冷却）而表里那个键没动：回写按 `(列, 参数名)` 逐项取数，
    取不到新值就不动论点。
    """
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = one(made, "stop.fixed")
    before = {"price": 28.0, "cooldown_min": 30}
    after = {"price": 28.0, "cooldown_min": 60}
    assert write_back(stop, before, after) == {}


def test_the_write_back_table_names_a_real_param_and_a_real_column() -> None:
    """回写表的两项都必须对得上别处：参数名得真是那个 kind 的模板参数，列名得真是论
    上的那一列。写错任何一项都不报错——`write_back` 取不到值，于是**静默不回写**，
    用户在屏幕上把止损线改到 30，论上还写着 28。这一枚把「表与模板同步」钉住。
    """
    thesis_fields = {f.name for f in fields(Thesis)}
    for kind, (column, param_key) in WRITE_BACK.items():
        names = {p.name for p in template_for(kind).params}
        assert param_key in names, f"{kind} 的参数里没有 {param_key}：{sorted(names)}"
        assert column in thesis_fields, f"论上没有 {column} 这一列"


def test_a_rule_that_is_not_derived_writes_back_nothing(theses, rules) -> None:
    """挂在自己心里的价上的一条规则，改了它不该动任何论点。"""
    mine = rules.create(
        USER,
        kind="stop.fixed",
        name="我自己的止损价",
        target_type="symbol",
        target_ref="A/601088",
        params={"price": 20.0},
    )
    assert mine.thesis_id is None
    assert write_back(mine, {"price": 20.0}, {"price": 18.0}) == {}


def test_editing_the_tiers_writes_back_no_target(theses, rules) -> None:
    """分档是比例不是价位：把 `0.5,1.0` 改成 `0.3,0.8` 不该动论上的 `target_price`。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1").created
    tier = one(made, "tp.tiered")
    assert write_back(tier, {"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"tiers": [0.3, 0.8]}) == {}
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_derive.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.derive'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/derive.py`

```python
"""§9.2 建仓派生：一条论 → 六条规则，加那张「改了参数回写哪一列」的表。

派生出来的规则**都挂在论上**（`target_type='thesis'`、`thesis_id` 非空）。这一个形状
决定了两件事：`RuleRepo.remove` 会拒删它们（§9.2「不可删除，只能停用」），而 `write_back`
只在这种规则上生效——用户自己建的一条 `price.cross_below` 里那个数是他心里的价，
不是他论点上的区间上沿，回写会把一条与他当前论点无关的东西写进他的论点。

只有两条规则抄了论上的数字（止损线、买区上沿），所以回写表只有两项。剩下四条读的是
论本身（`tp.tiered` / `thesis.odds_below` / `thesis.drift` 走 `Ask.thesis`，
`scheduled.reevaluate` 走披露日），改了论不必重派它们。

派生不写 `thesis_events`：`to_status` 非空，而派生不是一次状态跳转（`set_levels` 的注释
同一个理由）。它写的只有 `rules` 那几行：`RuleRepo.create` 不留修订史，`rule_revisions`
的第一份快照要等这条规则第一次被实质修改才有。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from sams.storage.repos.rules import Rule, RuleRepo
from sams.storage.repos.theses import Thesis, ThesisRepo

#: §9.2 那一行点名的默认阈值：赔率跌破它，这笔就值得重看一遍
ODDS_FLOOR = 1.5
#: 披露日之后几天内提醒复检。这个数不是这里定的：`core/rules.py` 里 `within_days`
#: 那个参数写着 default=5.0（单位「天」= 自然日），派生取的就是它
REEVAL_DAYS = 5
#: 分批止盈的两档：到半程与到目标（§10.2「分批提醒」的那个「分批」）。
#: 派生给的是串的形状，库里存下来读回的是校验过的列表——两种形状 `update` 都吃得下
TIERS = "0.5,1.0"

#: 改了这条规则的哪个参数 ⇒ 回写论上的哪一列。参数名列在表里，是因为它不全局统一：
#: `stop.fixed` 读 `price`，而 `stop.pct` / `stop.trailing` 那两种的键是 `pct`——
#: 只在这一处写死一列名的话，加一行就会静默不回写
WRITE_BACK = {
    "stop.fixed": ("stop_price", "price"),
    "price.cross_below": ("entry_high", "price"),
}

#: 派生这一条时默认带的过滤器：披露日窗口内每个交易日都会各判中一次，而 §10.1 的降噪
#: 取向是「同票已有一条没点掉的就不再推」。`templates.scheduled_reevaluate` 的 docstring
#: 承诺的就是这一行——不带它，那一段窗口天天响。
REEVAL_FILTERS = {"no_open_alert": True}

#: 只有这一条真的与账户有关（`tp.tiered` 要读那一笔的持仓成本），所以幂等键里只有它
#: 带账户；其余五条是论的事实，按账户复制就会让同一件事按账户发 N 条重复告警
PER_ACCOUNT = frozenset({"tp.tiered"})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Blueprint:
    """一条该建的规则（还没进库）。`reason` 是屏幕上那句「凭什么建这条」。"""

    kind: str
    name: str
    params: dict
    reason: str
    filters: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NotDerived:
    """这一条这次不建，并且说得出为什么——沉默的跳过就是「规则怎么少了两条」。"""

    kind: str
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeriveReport:
    """一次派生的回执：建了哪几条、哪些已存在、哪些这次不建（附理由）。给 T-17 的维护面
    与 T-18 的 `sams rule derive` 打印用，不是只给测试看的。"""

    thesis_id: str
    created: tuple[Rule, ...] = ()
    already: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    skipped: tuple[NotDerived, ...] = ()


def blueprints_for(
    thesis: Thesis, *, account_id: str | None = None
) -> tuple[Blueprint | NotDerived, ...]:
    """论上的哪一列派生出哪条规则，一次列全。缺列就是一条 `NotDerived`，不是静默少一条。"""
    name = thesis.code
    out: list[Blueprint | NotDerived] = []
    if thesis.entry_high is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("price.cross_below", "论上没有区间上沿：没有可盯的那条线"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "price.cross_below",
                f"{name} 跌进买入区间",
                {"price": thesis.entry_high},
                f"区间上沿 {thesis.entry_high:g}：从上方跌进来就是可执行计划",
            )
        )
    if thesis.stop_price is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("stop.fixed", "论上没有止损价：三级提醒没有那条线"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "stop.fixed",
                f"{name} 止损三级",
                {"price": thesis.stop_price},
                f"止损价 {thesis.stop_price:g}：pre / hit / urgent 三档由模板给",
            )
        )
    if thesis.target_price is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("tp.tiered", "论上没有目标价：分档没有终点"))
    elif account_id is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("tp.tiered", "没有账户：分批止盈要读这一笔的成本"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "tp.tiered",
                f"{name} 分批止盈",
                {"tiers": TIERS},
                f"目标价 {thesis.target_price:g}：到半程与到终点各提醒一次",
            )
        )
    if thesis.reward_risk is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("thesis.odds_below", "论上没有赔率：这条永远不会成立"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "thesis.odds_below",
                f"{name} 赔率跌破阈值",
                {"threshold": ODDS_FLOOR},
                f"现赔率 {thesis.reward_risk:g}：跌破 {ODDS_FLOOR:g} 就值得重看这笔",
            )
        )
    out.append(
        Blueprint(
            "scheduled.reevaluate",
            f"{name} 披露日复检",
            {"within_days": REEVAL_DAYS},
            "新一期财报落地后，论上的数就是旧数",
            filters=dict(REEVAL_FILTERS),
        )
    )
    out.append(
        Blueprint(
            "thesis.drift",
            f"{name} 观点漂移",
            {},
            "周复检掉一档就提醒（§9.3：只提示，不替谁决定平仓）",
        )
    )
    return tuple(out)


class RuleDeriver:
    """把蓝图落到库里。幂等：同一 (论, kind) 已存在就不再建——**跳过而不是覆盖**，
    用户改过的那条参数不能在一次重复派生里被悄悄写回蓝图值。

    幂等键上只有 `tp.tiered` 多带一个 `account_id`（`PER_ACCOUNT`）：同一只论在两个账户
    各有一笔、成本不同，那一档要各算一次；其余五条按 kind 幂等，重复派生不会按账户
    复制出 N 条同样的事实。
    """

    def __init__(self, rules: RuleRepo, theses: ThesisRepo) -> None:
        self.rules = rules
        self.theses = theses

    def derive(
        self, user_id: str, thesis_id: str, *, account_id: str | None = None
    ) -> DeriveReport:
        thesis = self.theses.require(user_id, thesis_id)
        have = {(row.kind, row.account_id) for row in self.rules.by_thesis(thesis_id)}
        created: list[Rule] = []
        already: list[str] = []
        skipped: list[NotDerived] = []
        for item in blueprints_for(thesis, account_id=account_id):
            if isinstance(item, NotDerived):
                skipped.append(item)
                continue
            owner = account_id if item.kind in PER_ACCOUNT else None
            if (item.kind, owner) in have:
                already.append(item.kind)
                continue
            created.append(
                self.rules.create(
                    user_id,
                    kind=item.kind,
                    name=item.name,
                    target_type="thesis",
                    target_ref=thesis_id,
                    params=item.params,
                    filters=item.filters,
                    account_id=owner,
                    thesis_id=thesis_id,
                )
            )
            have.add((item.kind, owner))
        return DeriveReport(
            thesis_id=thesis_id,
            created=tuple(created),
            already=tuple(already),
            skipped=tuple(skipped),
        )


def write_back(
    rule: Rule, before: Mapping[str, object], after: Mapping[str, object]
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """编辑一条派生规则时要回写论上的哪几列。空字典 = 不动任何论点（两种情况都常见）。"""
    if rule.thesis_id is None:
        return {}
    entry = WRITE_BACK.get(rule.kind)
    if entry is None:
        return {}
    column, param_key = entry
    value = after.get(param_key)
    if value is None or before.get(param_key) == value:
        return {}
    return {column: float(value)}
```

- [ ] **Step 4：跑绿 + 分层**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_derive.py tests/test_layering.py -q`
Expected: 全绿，**派生 21 枚 + 分层 366 枚**；`uv run pytest -m "not live" -q` 全库
**2515 passed, 14 deselected**。`derive.py` 只 import `storage`（它不读价、不读行情，只做形状），
这一条顺带说明为什么派生不需要 `analysis`，也不需要 `datasource`。

- [ ] **Step 5：提交并推远端**

```bash
git add src/sams/services/rules/derive.py tests/services/test_rules_derive.py
git commit -m "feat(rules): §9.2 建仓派生与回写表（六条蓝图、幂等、缺列要说）"
git fetch origin main && git merge --ff-only origin/main && git push origin main
```

**这一步会撞上的一个库外事实：** `RuleRepo` 与 `ThesisRepo` 都活着，但 `rules` 表在此之前
**一个写入方都没有**（`sams rule add` 是 T-18 才有的命令）。T-15 的测试因此是这个仓库里
第一次有代码往 `rules` 写行：如果 Step 4 报 `by_thesis` 返回空而不是六行，先查 `create` 那条
INSERT 的列顺序，再回头看派生逻辑——两边都绿了才说明这一枚文件是对的。

**评审收尾改掉的那六处（实现与用例一起进 `e71b2a1`，不是另起一次提交）：**

1. **docstring 里那句关于 `rule_revisions` 的假话。** 原写「它写的是 `rules` 那几行与
   `rule_revisions` 的第一份快照」——`RuleRepo.create` 不留修订史，只有 `update` 才写。
   改成「第一份快照要等这条规则第一次被实质修改才有」。计划原文（本文件 Step 3 之外的
   说明）没有重复这句话，所以只需改代码里那一段。
2. **`REEVAL_DAYS` 的出处。** 原注释把它说成「§9.3 周复检节奏里一周的工作日数」：这个数
   其实是 `core/rules.py` 里 `within_days` 那个参数的 `default=5.0`，单位是自然日。
3. **阈值断言改成字面值。** 原来写 `== ODDS_FLOOR`：把常量改成 2.5、`REEVAL_DAYS` 改成 7，
   21 枚全绿。现在断 `params["threshold"] == 1.5`、`within_days == 5`、`"跌破 1.5"` 上屏——
   那两个数是给用户的承诺，不是可以自由漂移的实现细节。
4. **补「那两个价是从哪一列抄的」。** 把 `price.cross_below` 的值换成 `thesis.stop_price`
   原先没人变红，而 `write_back` 按 kind 取列：抄错列会把目标价写进论的 `stop_price`，
   屏幕上那句话还说得漂漂亮亮。现在蓝图层与入库层各钉一枚（改动后 2 枚变红）。
5. **`scheduled.reevaluate` 带上 `no_open_alert`。** `templates.scheduled_reevaluate` 的
   docstring 明写「T-15 派生这一条时默认带」：不带就是披露日那段窗口天天响（§10.1 降噪）。
   `Blueprint` 因此多一个 `filters` 字段，`create` 那一调用带上它。
6. **幂等键上只有 `tp.tiered` 多带 `account_id`（`PER_ACCOUNT`）。** 原先六个都写 `account_id`：
   同一论在第二个账户上再派生会**一条都不建**，而分批止盈那一档读的是这一笔的成本、必须
   各建一条；反过来把其余五条也按账户复制，就是同一件事发 N 条重复告警。`WRITE_BACK` 的值
   同时改成 `(列名, 参数名)`：`stop.pct` / `stop.trailing` 的键是 `pct` 不是 `price`，
   只写一列名的话，以后加的那一行会静默不回写。

---

### T-16：`recheck.py`——§9.3 周复检：建基线、比基线，以及那两句不许说的谎

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/recheck.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py`

**这一枚只做数据侧，投递那一路仍然只有引擎一个出口。三个理由写在这：**

1. **判据只有一份。** 掉档的判定是 T-11 的 `thesis_drift`，复检调用的是同一个函数、同一个
   `Ask`，不重写第二份循环。两份「掉一档」的定义早晚分叉，而分叉的症状是每周复检说没掉、
   规则说掉了，用户不知道信哪个。
2. **复检只发事件，不发消息。** `ThesisDrifted` 是一条记录；「观点漂移，请复核」那句话由
   §9.2 派生出来的 `thesis.drift` 规则在 `rule check` 那一趟命中后投出去（走冷却、走过滤、
   走 D95 那个端口）。两边都投 = 同一件事每周响两遍，而防刷屏是这套系统的生死线（§10.1）。
3. **没有基线的时候必须建，而不是比。** 基线是「建仓时那一眼」，而那一眼在 M2-D 之前没人
   存过（0016 那三列是空的）。第一次跑复检若不写基线，`thesis.drift` 就永远停在「还没有
   基线」上，那条派生规则从此不响——屏幕上它是启用的。

**D96 那两句话在这个文件里各有一枚用例钉着：**「从没比过」不等于「没漂移」（没基线就
不比，只建），反过来「这一趟没算出分」也不等于「没掉档」（有基线但读不出分，那句谎同样
不许说）。合成分被拒的唯一理由是覆盖率不到地板（D70），那是数据问题，不是观点没变。

命令行出口在 T-18 的 `sams rule recheck`；这一枚交付的是服务与那条事件。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py`

```python
"""§9.3：opened/tracking 的论每周重算一次，第一次只建基线，之后才比。

用例对着 `support_valuation` 那个世界跑（300 天日线 + 两期年报 + 逐日估值，时钟冻在
`DAYS[-1]`），因为「这一趟到底有没有分」是那条链的回答，不是这个文件的回答。基线用
`set_baseline` 手写到已知档位上，掉档的那几项因此可以逐字断言。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta

import pytest
import support_valuation

from sams.core.errors import StorageError, ValidationError
from sams.core.events import EventBus, ThesisDrifted
from sams.services.rules.recheck import (
    BASELINE,
    CLEAN,
    DRIFT,
    NO_SCORE,
    PARTIAL,
    Rechecker,
)
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Target
from sams.storage.repos.theses import OPENED, PROPOSED, TRACKING

CODE = support_valuation.CODE
AS_OF = support_valuation.DAYS[-1]
USER = "u-recheck"
T0 = datetime(2024, 10, 26, 15, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
LEVELS = {
    "entry_low": 30.0,
    "entry_high": 36.0,
    "stop_price": 28.0,
    "target_price": 52.0,
    "reward_risk": 2.0,
}


class Recorder:
    """订阅 `ThesisDrifted`：这一枚文件的验收线有一半在「事件发没发」上。"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events: list[ThesisDrifted] = []

    def __call__(self, event: ThesisDrifted) -> None:
        self.events.append(event)


@pytest.fixture
def container(db, settings):
    return support_valuation.make_container(db, settings)


def build(container, bus=None) -> Rechecker:
    # 取数装配用容器那头拼好的 `rule_data`（`bootstrap.py` 是接线的唯一出处）
    return Rechecker(
        theses=container.theses_store,
        data=container.rule_data,
        bus=bus or EventBus(),
        clock=container.clock,
    )


def open_thesis(container, *, status: str = OPENED, code: str = CODE):
    theses = container.theses_store
    thesis = theses.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code=code,
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
        **LEVELS,
    )
    theses.advance(USER, thesis.id, OPENED, actor_id=USER, now=T0)
    if status == TRACKING:
        theses.advance(USER, thesis.id, TRACKING, actor_id=USER, now=T0)
    return theses.get(USER, thesis.id)


def lay_baseline(container, thesis, items, score=95.0) -> None:
    """把基线写成已知的那几档（`on` 故意写成比复检日更旧的一个日子；`RecheckRow` 不
    带这个日期，新旧只看库）。"""
    container.theses_store.set_baseline(
        USER, thesis.id, score=score, items=items, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
    )


# --- 第一次跑：建基线，不比 ----------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_first_run_lays_a_baseline_instead_of_judging(container) -> None:
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [BASELINE]
    row = report.rows[0]
    assert row.written is True  # 真跑的这一趟确实写了库
    assert row.score is not None  # 那个世界的合成分确实出得来
    assert row.baseline_score == row.score
    assert "下一次" in row.message
    # 基线落库了，而且属于复检那一天
    stored = container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)
    assert stored.baseline_score == row.score
    assert stored.baseline_on == AS_OF


def test_a_second_run_compares_instead_of_reevaluating_the_baseline(container) -> None:
    """基线已经在了：第二趟必须走比较那一路，不许把基线刷成「今天」。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    rechecker = build(container)
    rechecker.run(USER)
    first = container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)

    again = rechecker.run(USER)
    stored = container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)

    assert [row.state for row in again.rows] == [CLEAN]
    assert stored.baseline_on == first.baseline_on


def test_a_thesis_without_a_score_gets_no_baseline_and_says_so(db, settings) -> None:
    """拔掉基本面 → 覆盖不到地板 → 拒分（D70）。这时既不能建基线，也不能说「没漂移」。"""
    thin = support_valuation.make_container(db, settings, with_fundamentals=False)
    thesis = open_thesis(thin)
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(thin, bus).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [NO_SCORE]
    assert report.rows[0].score is None
    assert "覆盖" in report.rows[0].message
    assert thin.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id).baseline_score is None
    assert rec.events == []


def test_a_thesis_that_cannot_be_read_does_not_stop_the_run(container) -> None:
    """一只没有日线的票（退市或从没同步）：取数那头拒绝的是**这一条**，整趟不该被它打掉，
    而屏幕上那条要说「读不到」——一个空白行会被读成复检过了。"""
    read_ok = open_thesis(container)
    blind = container.theses_store.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="600000",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
        **LEVELS,
    )
    container.theses_store.advance(USER, blind.id, OPENED, actor_id=USER, now=T0)

    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert {row.code: row.state for row in report.rows} == {CODE: BASELINE, "600000": NO_SCORE}
    row = next(r for r in report.rows if r.code == "600000")
    assert "读不到" in row.message
    assert "「读不到」不是「没漂移」" in row.message
    assert container.theses_store.get(USER, blind.id).baseline_score is None
    assert container.theses_store.get(USER, read_ok.id).baseline_score is not None


def test_dry_run_writes_nothing_but_still_says_what_it_would_do(container) -> None:
    open_thesis(container)
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER, dry_run=True)

    assert report.dry_run is True
    assert report.rows[0].state == BASELINE
    assert report.rows[0].written is False
    assert "试运行" in report.rows[0].message
    assert report.count(BASELINE) == 1
    # 屏幕上说的是「会建」，库里那三列一个字没动
    assert container.theses_store.list(USER)[0].baseline_score is None
    assert rec.events == []


# --- 之后每次：比基线 ---------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_a_drop_of_one_tier_publishes_the_event_and_names_the_item(container) -> None:
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    # 这一趟那个世界读到 pr 58.455、roe 85.0：80 与 85 同在 4 档，所以只有 PR 掉档
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"pr": 80.0, "roe": 80.0})
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [DRIFT]
    assert report.drifted[0].thesis_id == thesis.id
    assert "市赚率 PR" in report.rows[0].message
    assert "净资产收益率" not in report.rows[0].message  # 没掉的那一项不进这句话
    assert [event.thesis_id for event in rec.events] == [thesis.id]
    assert "复核" in rec.events[0].detail
    assert "只提示" in rec.events[0].detail  # §9.3：系统不替谁决定平仓


def test_a_drop_inside_one_tier_reads_back_as_compared_and_clean(container) -> None:
    """40 → 58 在同一档里（20 分一档，D 那一头的理由）：跨档才算变了。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"pr": 40.0})
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [CLEAN]
    assert rec.events == []


def test_a_drop_on_a_dry_run_is_shown_but_not_published(container) -> None:
    """试运行那一趟屏幕上照旧说「漂移」，但一条事件都不许发：事件是「发生了什么」的记录，
    试运行里什么都没发生——发出去的每一条都是在记一件没发生过的事。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"pr": 80.0})
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER, dry_run=True)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [DRIFT]
    assert report.rows[0].written is False
    assert "试运行" in report.rows[0].message
    assert rec.events == []


def test_an_item_the_run_did_not_compute_is_named_not_skipped(container) -> None:
    """基线里有主力净流入、这一趟那一路没数：这句必须是「比不全」，不是「没掉档」。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"main_inflow": 90.0})

    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [PARTIAL]
    assert "主力净流入" in report.rows[0].message
    assert "没有掉档" not in report.rows[0].message


def test_a_thesis_with_no_baseline_is_never_reported_as_not_drifted(container) -> None:
    """D96 的那句谎单独钉一次：没有基线时屏幕上出现的那个词只能是「建立基线」。"""
    open_thesis(container)
    report = build(container).run(USER)

    for row in report.rows:
        assert row.state != CLEAN
        assert "未漂移" not in row.message


def test_items_are_still_compared_when_the_run_produced_no_total_score(db, settings) -> None:
    """已有基线、这一趟没出总分：分项照样比——`thesis_drift` 判的是分项，不是 `score`。

    拔掉基本面的那个世界实测给的就是这个形状（`score=None` + 四个有数的分项，覆盖不到
    地板只拒合成），基线铺在这四个键上，比出来是「比过了」而不是任何一句谎。
    """
    thin = support_valuation.make_container(db, settings, with_fundamentals=False)
    thesis = open_thesis(thin)
    snapshot = thin.rule_data.read(Target(USER, "A", CODE, None, thesis.id), as_of=AS_OF)
    assert snapshot.score is None and snapshot.items  # 这一枚用例押的就是这个形状
    lay_baseline(thin, thesis, dict(snapshot.items))

    report = build(thin).run(USER)
    row = report.rows[0]

    assert row.state == CLEAN
    assert row.score is None
    assert "总分这一趟没出" in row.message
    # 基线一个字没刷：还是铺下去的那个数、那一天的那笔
    stored = thin.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)
    assert stored.baseline_score == 95.0
    assert stored.baseline_on == date(2024, 7, 1)


# --- 谁在复检范围内 -----------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_recheck_walks_opened_and_tracking_only(container) -> None:
    theses = container.theses_store
    # `600000` 没有日线，出行靠读失败那一支——在不在名单里与读不读得到是两件事
    open_thesis(container, code="600000")
    open_thesis(container, status=TRACKING)
    idle = theses.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="000800",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
    )

    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert {(row.code, row.status) for row in report.rows} == {
        ("600000", OPENED),
        (CODE, TRACKING),
    }
    assert theses.get(USER, idle.id).baseline_score is None


def test_a_named_thesis_is_rechecked_alone(container) -> None:
    first = open_thesis(container)
    # 同一个代码换一种风格：`(user, market, code, style)` 不同才允许两条同时活着
    second = container.theses_store.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code=CODE,
        style="trend",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
        **LEVELS,
    )
    container.theses_store.advance(USER, second.id, OPENED, actor_id=USER, now=T0)

    report = build(container).run(USER, thesis_id=second.id)

    assert [row.thesis_id for row in report.rows] == [second.id]
    assert report.rows[0].state == BASELINE
    assert container.theses_store.get(USER, first.id).baseline_score is None


def test_a_named_thesis_outside_the_two_statuses_is_refused_with_a_reason(container) -> None:
    """未建仓的论没有「建仓时那一眼」可比：静默返回零行会被读成复检过了。"""
    idle = container.theses_store.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="600000",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="opened"):
        build(container).run(USER, thesis_id=idle.id)


def test_an_unknown_thesis_says_unknown(container) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(StorageError, match="thesis"):
        build(container).run(USER, thesis_id="does-not-exist")


def test_the_default_day_is_the_clock_today(container) -> None:
    report = build(container).run(USER)
    assert report.as_of == container.clock.today()


def test_an_explicit_as_of_is_stamped_into_the_write_once_baseline(container) -> None:
    """`as_of` 会落进 `baseline_on`，而那一列写一次就不让改（`set_baseline` 拒第二笔）：
    这一天传错了，基线的日子就永远错着，所以传进来的那天必须原样落库。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    day = AS_OF - timedelta(days=7)

    report = build(container).run(USER, as_of=day)

    assert report.as_of == day
    assert container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id).baseline_on == day
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.recheck'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/recheck.py`

```python
"""§9.3 周复检：给每条 `opened`/`tracking` 的论重算一次分，再决定这一次能不能比。

判据不在这里——掉档的判定是 `templates.thesis_drift`，复检调的是同一个函数、同一个 `Ask`。
两份「掉一档」的定义早晚分叉，而分叉的症状是每周复检说没掉、规则说掉了。

复检**只发事件，不发消息**：`ThesisDrifted` 是一条记录，「观点漂移，请复核」那句话由
§9.2 派生的 `thesis.drift` 规则在 `rule check` 那一趟命中后投出去（走过滤、走冷却、走 D95
那个端口）。两边都投就是同一件事每周响两遍，而防刷屏是这套系统的生死线（§10.1）。

第一次跑必须**建**基线而不是比：基线是「建仓时那一眼」，而那三列（`baseline_*`）是 0016
才加上的，加上的时候每一行都是空的。不写基线，`thesis.drift` 就永远停在「还没有基线」，
那条派生规则从此不响，而派生给一条论时它默认是启用的（`RuleRepo.create` 的 `enabled`）。

两句谎都不许说（D96）：没有基线 ≠ 没漂移；这一趟没算出分（覆盖不到地板，D70）也 ≠ 没掉档。
前者这一趟只建、不比。没有分的时候不建基线——那三列写一次，钉错了改不回来；但分项照样比，
因为 `thesis_drift` 判的是分项：一条已有基线的论即使这趟没出总分，也比，也可能报漂移。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date

from sams.core.clock import SYSTEM_CLOCK, Clock
from sams.core.errors import NotAdjustable, ValidationError
from sams.core.events import EventBus, ThesisDrifted
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import RuleData, Snapshot, Target
from sams.services.rules.templates import Ask, thesis_drift
from sams.storage.repos.theses import OPENED, TRACKING, Thesis, ThesisRepo

#: 复检只走这两档：`proposed`/`observing` 还没有「建仓时那一眼」，
#: `hit_*`/`invalidated`/`archived` 已经过去了（`set_baseline` 那头也拒归档的那条）
RECHECK_STATUSES = (OPENED, TRACKING)

BASELINE = "baseline"
DRIFT = "drift"
CLEAN = "clean"
PARTIAL = "partial"
NO_SCORE = "no_score"

#: 屏幕上那一列的词（T-18 的 `rule recheck` 打的是这张表）。五个状态说的都是「这一趟
#: 做了什么」，不是「这条论现在怎么样」——后者是那条论的状态机在回答，这里不越权
STATE_NAMES = {
    BASELINE: "建立基线",
    DRIFT: "观点漂移",
    CLEAN: "比过了",
    PARTIAL: "比不全",
    NO_SCORE: "还没有分",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RecheckRow:
    """一条论的一次复检。`message` 是屏幕上那一行，`state` 是它属于哪一类。"""

    thesis_id: str
    user_id: str
    market: str
    code: str
    name: str
    status: str
    state: str
    #: 复检**之后**库里那条基线（这一趟没写它就是原值；写了 BASELINE 那一行的就是刚落库的数）
    baseline_score: float | None
    score: float | None
    message: str
    #: 这一趟有没有写库（发事件不算）
    written: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RecheckReport:
    user_id: str
    as_of: date
    dry_run: bool
    rows: tuple[RecheckRow, ...] = ()

    def count(self, state: str) -> int:
        return sum(1 for row in self.rows if row.state == state)

    @property
    def drifted(self) -> tuple[RecheckRow, ...]:
        return tuple(row for row in self.rows if row.state == DRIFT)


class Rechecker:
    """一周一次的维护动作：建基线、比基线、把掉档记成事件。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        theses: ThesisRepo,
        data: RuleData,
        bus: EventBus,
        clock: Clock = SYSTEM_CLOCK,
    ) -> None:
        self.theses = theses
        self.data = data
        self.bus = bus
        self.clock = clock

    def run(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        *,
        as_of: date | None = None,
        dry_run: bool = False,
        thesis_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> RecheckReport:
        day = as_of if as_of is not None else self.clock.today()
        targets = self._select(user_id, thesis_id)
        rows = tuple(self._one(thesis, day, dry_run) for thesis in targets)
        return RecheckReport(user_id=user_id, as_of=day, dry_run=dry_run, rows=rows)

    def _select(self, user_id: str, thesis_id: str | None) -> list[Thesis]:
        if thesis_id is None:
            found = list(self.theses.list(user_id, status=OPENED))
            found += list(self.theses.list(user_id, status=TRACKING))
            return found
        thesis = self.theses.require(user_id, thesis_id)
        if thesis.status not in RECHECK_STATUSES:
            allowed = "/".join(RECHECK_STATUSES)
            raise ValidationError(
                f"{thesis.id} 现在是 {thesis.status}，不在复检范围内（只比 {allowed}）："
                "没建过仓的论没有「建仓时那一眼」可比，静默返回零行会被读成复检过了"
            )
        return [thesis]

    def _one(self, thesis: Thesis, as_of: date, dry_run: bool) -> RecheckRow:
        target = Target(thesis.user_id, thesis.market, thesis.code, None, thesis.id)
        try:
            snapshot = self.data.read(target, as_of=as_of)
        except NotAdjustable as exc:
            # 与引擎同一句理由（T-14 那一轮补的）：一条读不到的论不该把整趟周复检打掉——
            # 打掉的代价是剩下的那些论这一周没人看。这一条记成「还没有分」，把读不出分的原因
            # 原样带出去，而不是留下一个空白行让人以为复检过
            return _row(
                thesis,
                NO_SCORE,
                f"这一趟读不到这个对象（{exc}）：基线与比较都等读到再说，"
                "「读不到」不是「没漂移」",
                name=thesis.code,
                score=None,
            )
        if thesis.baseline_score is None:
            return self._lay_baseline(thesis, snapshot, as_of, dry_run)
        return self._compare(thesis, snapshot, dry_run)

    def _lay_baseline(
        self, thesis: Thesis, snapshot: Snapshot, as_of: date, dry_run: bool
    ) -> RecheckRow:
        if snapshot.score is None:
            return _row(
                thesis,
                NO_SCORE,
                "这一趟没合成出分（分项覆盖不到地板，D70）：基线与比较都等它有分再说——"
                "现在写下的「基线」会把一条论钉死在一个永远达不到的数上",
                name=snapshot.name,
                score=None,
            )
        if not dry_run:
            self.theses.set_baseline(
                thesis.user_id,
                thesis.id,
                score=snapshot.score,
                items=dict(snapshot.items),
                on=as_of,
                now=self.clock.now_utc(),
            )
        return _row(
            thesis,
            BASELINE,
            f"基线建立：{snapshot.score:g} 分（{as_of}，{len(snapshot.items)} 个分项）。"
            "这一趟只记下「当时那一眼」，下一次复检才有得比"
            + ("（试运行：没有写库）" if dry_run else ""),
            name=snapshot.name,
            score=snapshot.score,
            written=not dry_run,
            # 复检之后库里那条：写了就是刚落库这个数；干跑什么都没写，还是那条空的
            baseline_score=None if dry_run else snapshot.score,
        )

    def _compare(self, thesis: Thesis, snapshot: Snapshot, dry_run: bool) -> RecheckRow:
        got = thesis_drift(Ask(snapshot=snapshot, params={}, thesis=thesis))
        if got.hit is not None:
            if not dry_run:
                self.bus.publish(
                    ThesisDrifted(
                        thesis_id=thesis.id,
                        user_id=thesis.user_id,
                        detail=got.hit.why,
                        ts=self.clock.now_utc(),
                    )
                )
            return _row(
                thesis,
                DRIFT,
                got.hit.why + ("（试运行：没有发事件）" if dry_run else ""),
                name=snapshot.name,
                score=snapshot.score,
                written=False,
            )
        if got.why_not:
            return _row(
                thesis,
                PARTIAL,
                got.why_not,
                name=snapshot.name,
                score=snapshot.score,
            )
        now_text = (
            f"{snapshot.score:g} 分" if snapshot.score is not None else "总分这一趟没出"
        )
        return _row(
            thesis,
            CLEAN,
            f"比过了：基线 {thesis.baseline_score:g} 分，{now_text}，分项没有掉档",
            name=snapshot.name,
            score=snapshot.score,
        )


def _row(
    thesis: Thesis,
    state: str,
    message: str,
    *,
    name: str,
    score: float | None,
    written: bool = False,
    baseline_score: float | None = None,
) -> RecheckRow:
    # `baseline_score` 不给就用论上那条：这一趟没动基线的行，库里那条就是复检之后那条
    return RecheckRow(
        thesis_id=thesis.id,
        user_id=thesis.user_id,
        market=thesis.market,
        code=thesis.code,
        name=name,
        status=thesis.status,
        state=state,
        baseline_score=(
            thesis.baseline_score if baseline_score is None else baseline_score
        ),
        score=score,
        message=message,
        written=written,
    )
```

- [ ] **Step 4：跑绿 + 分层**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py tests/test_layering.py -q`
Expected: 全绿，**复检 17 枚 + 分层 369 枚**；`uv run pytest -m "not live" -q` 全库
**2535 passed, 14 deselected**。分项的名字（`市赚率 PR`、`主力净流入`）由 `thesis_drift` 写进
它自己那两句里，`recheck.py` 一个字都不拼——所以这个文件不 import `analysis`，它只 import
`core`、`storage` 和同层的 `snapshot`/`templates`。

**评审收尾改掉的那几处（实现与用例一起提交，不是另起一次）：**

1. **计划自己那枚用例按原样跑不红也不对。** 基线写 `{"pr": 80.0, "roe": 100.0}` 时，
   `roe` 在这个世界实测 **85.0**（4 档），而 100.0 是 5 档——`净资产收益率` **会**进那句漂移
   的话，正好撞上面上一行的 `assert "净资产收益率" not in ...`。改成 `roe: 80.0`（80 与 85
   同在 4 档），断言一个字没松。
2. **「没有分这一趟就不比」是一句 overstated 的话。** 实测：拔掉基本面那个世界给
   `score=None` **加四个有数的分项**（`scoring.py:437` 只拒合成，不扔分项）。所以「不比」只在
   建基线那一路成立，比基线那一路判的是**分项**——已有基线的论即使这趟没总分也比、也可能报
   漂移。docstring 改成那句真的，并补一枚用例把这一支钉住（原形状在套件里没人走到过）。
3. **`NotAdjustable` 那一支是补的**：一只没有日线的票（退市/从没同步）在取数那头抛的正是
   这一种，不兜就是整趟周复检被一条论打掉。取向与 `engine.py:266` 同一句理由，复用已有的
   `NO_SCORE` 状态，不新增状态也不新增出口。
4. **三枚「永远不可能红」的断言换掉了。** `"没掉档" not in ...` 挡的是「分项**没有**掉档」那句
   ——子串对不上，改动它也不会变色；一枚改成 `没有掉档`，另一枚改成正向断言那句免责声明
   本身。`written` 此前没有任何用例断过 `True`，现在真跑那一趟断 `written is True`。
5. **枚名与内容不符的那一枚重建了。** `walks_opened_and_tracking_only` 原来只建了一条
   `TRACKING`：把 `OPENED` 那一半从名单里删掉（`found = []`）它照样绿。现在 `OPENED` 与
   `TRACKING` 各一条，断的是 `(code, status)` 那一个集合。
6. **`as_of` 此前零覆盖**：写死成「时钟今天」全库无人变色。而它是落进 `baseline_on` 的那一天，
   那一列写一次就不让改（`theses.py:454`）——传错一天就永远错着。补一枚传 7 天前的用例。
7. **D96 的三处文案**：`0016 那三列是空的` 改成「那一支迁移 `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`，加上的
   时候每一行都是空的」；用例里那句「订阅 `ThesisDrifted` 的 `thesis.drift` 会推给用户」删掉
   （`src/` 里没有任何订阅方，`thesis.drift` 是引擎在 `rule check` 里重算的模板）；`lay_baseline`
   那句「屏幕能分辨新旧」删掉（`RecheckRow` 没有 `baseline_on` 这一列）。
8. **删掉一个没人读的 API**：`RecheckRow.drifted`（只有 `RecheckReport.drifted` 在用）。
   `baseline_score` 那个字段改成统一意思「复检**之后**库里那条基线」，于是 `replace(...)` 与它
   那句自我豁免的注释一起消失。测试的 `build()` 改用 `container.rule_data`（`bootstrap.py:283`
   是这份接线的唯一出处，手写那份是第三份副本）。
